### [1] [X-CoSD: Communication-Efficient Cross-Vocabulary Collaborative Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09166)
*Jaeduk Lee,Wan Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出跨词表协作投机解码（X-CoSD）及其增强版 X-CoSD-E，解决异构词表下的通信开销问题，通过混合重采样（HR）和服务端重采样-设备验证（SR-DV）实现无损分布保持与加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作投机解码（CoSD）假设小模型与大模型共享词表，且残差重采样需要在设备与边缘服务器间交换 Token 分布，导致巨大通信负载。

Method: 1. 提出 X-CoSD：基于混合重采样（HR），将残差重采样拆分为设备端的共同词表区域与服务端的大模型专属区域，仅需传输共同词表区域的分布。2. 提出 X-CoSD-E：基于服务端重采样-设备验证（SR-DV），服务端仅发送采样的替换候选及其概率，由设备端本地验证。

Result: 理论证明 X-CoSD 与 X-CoSD-E 均能保持服务端大模型的原始分布；实验显示两者显著提升 Token 生成速度，且生成质量与服务端大模型相当。

Conclusion: X-CoSD 框架在异构词表条件下实现了无损、通信高效的协作投机解码，为设备-云协同大模型推理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates collaborative speculative decoding (CoSD), a distributed large language model (LLM) inference framework in which an on-device small language model (SLM) drafts candidate tokens and a server LLM verifies them. Existing CoSD methods assume a shared vocabulary between the SLM and the LLM and incur substantial communication load because residual resampling requires token distribution exchange between the user device and the edge server. To address these limitations, we propose cross-vocabulary CoSD (X-CoSD), a lossless and communication-efficient CoSD framework for heterogeneous SLM-LLM vocabularies. X-CoSD is built on hybrid resampling (HR), which splits residual resampling across the common-vocabulary region on the device and the LLM-only region on the server, so that distribution transmission is required only for the common-vocabulary region. We further propose X-CoSD-E, an enhanced variant based on server resampling with device verification (SR-DV), in which the server sends only replacement candidates sampled from the server LLM and their corresponding probabilities for local verification at the device. We prove that both X-CoSD and X-CoSD-E preserve the server LLM distribution, and experiments show that they significantly improve token generation speed while maintaining generation quality comparable to that of the server LLM.

</details>


### [2] [StochBench: A Domain-Specific Benchmark for Stochastic Processes in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09264)
*Idan Davidovich,Debargha Ganguly,Vikash Singh,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出StochBench，一个包含450个研究生水平随机过程问题的Lean 4基准测试，涵盖马尔可夫链、马尔丁格尔、布朗运动等主题，配有自然语言源文本。基于Opus 4.8的代理在每题15分钟限制下达成34.9%证明率，该基准更好地代表领域特定应用数学，同时对先进证明器仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化定理证明基准主要源自竞赛数学（如IMO、Putnam），规模小且不能代表领域特定应用。随机过程作为应用数学核心领域，在Mathlib中代表性不足，缺乏系统性基准测试。

Method: 构建StochBench基准：收集450个研究生级随机过程问题，涵盖有限/可数马尔可夫链、更新过程、随机游走、马尔丁格尔、停时、排队论、布朗运动、随机微积分、弱收敛、泊松及连续时间马尔可夫过程等主题。每个问题配对自然语言源文本，形式化为Lean 4语言。使用基于Opus 4.8的代理进行评测，设定每题15分钟时间限制。

Result: Opus 4.8代理在450个问题中证明了157个，证明率34.9%。基准覆盖多个抽象层级，展示了当前大语言模型在领域特定随机过程形式化证明上的能力边界。

Conclusion: StochBench填补了随机过程领域形式化基准的空白，比竞赛数学基准更好地代表应用数学领域特定问题。尽管证明率仅34.9%，但为推进领域特定定理证明提供了具有挑战性且具代表性的测试平台。

Abstract: Leading benchmarks for formal theorem proving with large language models are small collections drawn from competition math, such as the IMO and Putnam, that poorly represent field-specific applications. We introduce StochBench, a Lean 4 benchmark of 450 graduate stochastic-processes problems at varying abstraction levels, each paired with its natural-language source. Addressing a field underrepresented in Mathlib, it covers finite and countable Markov chains, renewal processes, random walks, martingales, stopping times, queues, Brownian motion, stochastic calculus, weak convergence, and Poisson and continuous-time Markov processes. Our Opus 4.8-based agent achieves a 34.9% proof rate (157/450) under a 15-minute per-problem limit. StochBench better represents domain-specific applied mathematics while remaining challenging for advanced provers.

</details>


### [3] [From Retrieval to Weights: Parametric Individualization of Small Language Models with Individual Text Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10155)
*Christoph Wigbels,Ali Abusaleh,Markus T. Jansen,Alexander Mehler,Markus J. Hofmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种认知模拟视角，将个体文本语料（ITC）整合到检索增强生成和DoRA微调中，模拟个体在多项选择题回答中的情景记忆与语义记忆。通过爬取515名参与者的搜索历史并分析150人分层子样本，为每人训练一个DoRA适配器将其ITC整合至小语言模型（SLM）。结果显示适配器能将ITC写入模型权重，呈现显著个体化效应；但在泛化知识测试中，适配器增加的是通用知识而非个体对齐，为个性化辅导智能体提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在从认知模拟角度探索如何将个体的情景记忆和语义记忆整合到语言模型中，实现个性化的多项选择题回答模拟，并为开发个性化辅导智能体奠定基础。

Method: 1) 爬取515名参与者回答36道多项选择题时的搜索历史，构建个体文本语料（ITC）；2) 分层抽样150名参与者；3) 为每位参与者训练一个DoRA适配器，将其ITC整合到基线正确率低于参与者最低四分位数的小语言模型（SLM）中；4) 评估适配器对个体文本的拟合程度（个体化效应）及在泛化知识测试上的表现（log-loss匹配、偏差校正PMI读出下的匹配准确率、检索增强效果）。

Result: 1) DoRA适配器成功将ITC写入模型权重：对自身参与者的held-out文本拟合优于其他参与者文本（dz=1.27），个体化效应随ITC规模按秩序增加；2) 泛化知识测试中，适配器提升了log-loss匹配，但偏差校正PMI读出下的匹配准确率未提升，检索增强无额外增益，表明适配器增加的是通用知识而非个体对齐。

Conclusion: ITC可整合至SLM权重中，为个性化辅导智能体提供了有希望的基础；未来工作需进一步探索如何实现个体层面情景记忆与语义记忆的真实模拟。

Abstract: We approach a cognitive simulation perspective on episodic and semantic memory in multiple-choice question answering by incorporating text from individual text corpora (ITC) into retrieval-augmented generation and DoRA fine-tuning. We web-crawl the search histories of 515 participants who answered 36 multiple-choice knowledge items and analyze a stratified subsample of 150 participants. For each participant, one DoRA adapter consolidates their ITC into a small language model (SLM) whose baseline correctness falls below the participants' lowest quartile. The adapter measurably writes the ITC into the weights: it fits its own participant's held-out text better than other participants' texts (dz =1.27), an individuality effect that increases with ITC size in rank order. On the generalized knowledge test, however, the adapter adds knowledge rather than alignment with the individual: log-loss match improves, whereas match accuracy under a bias-corrected PMI readout does not, and retrieval adds nothing on top. Our results demonstrate that ITCs can be consolidated into the weights of SLMs, an encouraging basis for individualized tutoring agents, and we discuss how to move from there toward a realistic simulation of episodic and semantic memory at the individual level.

</details>


### [4] [Osprey: Target-agnostic Pre-training Makes Stronger Drafters in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09338)
*Fengxiang Bie,Yuqing Jian,Yifan Yu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Zelei Shao,Ben Athiwaratkun,Shuaiwen Leon Song,Chenfeng Xu,Xiaoxia Wu,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Osprey 通过从预训练的小语言模型引导草稿模型，实现了可复用的、与目标模型无关的草稿预训练，仅需轻量级适配即可迁移到不同目标模型，显著提升了推测解码的接受率和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测解码草稿模型通常针对单一目标模型在窄分布上训练，导致在负载分布偏移时接受率崩塌。这与现代大语言模型通过大规模预训练获得广泛泛化能力的趋势背道而驰。虽然预训练是自然的补救措施，但现有方法要求草稿模型消费目标模型的隐藏状态并蒸馏其 logits，导致预训练必须为每个目标模型重复进行，无法复用。

Method: Osprey 从现成的预训练小语言模型引导草稿模型，将广泛的预训练视为可复用的目标无关资产。解决两大挑战：1) 小语言模型深度过大，不满足延迟敏感型草稿模型的需求——通过剪枝得到浅层骨干网络；2) 预训练能力必须保持完好，同时草稿模型需学习摄取目标隐状态并输出目标词表 token——通过目标无关的下一词预训练恢复语言建模能力，再通过词表对齐、零初始化的 QKV 扩展以及从目标模型输出分布蒸馏进行轻量级目标适配。

Result: 单个预训练的 Osprey 骨干网络可迁移至多个目标模型：在 Qwen3-8B 上平均接受长度提升 16.1%，在 Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct 上提升 21.2%，在 229B MiniMax-M2.5 上提升 22.7%（tokens per second 提高 17.5%）。在域外和多语言数据上增益最大。

Conclusion: Osprey 证明了可复用的草稿模型预训练是可行的，将针对每个目标模型的高昂预训练成本降低为一次性预训练加轻量级适配，显著提升了推测解码在多样化目标模型和工作负载下的鲁棒性与效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding is critical for accelerating LLM inference. However, the speedup is fragile: drafters are typically trained against a narrow distribution for a single target model, and their acceptance rate collapses under workload shifts. This is a striking inversion of modern LLM development, where target models are valued precisely for the broad generalization they acquire through large-scale pretraining. We argue that the natural remedy, pretraining, has been hard to apply to drafters because existing recipes are target-specific: the drafter consumes the target's hidden states and is distilled on the target's logits, so pretraining must be repeated for each target. We introduce Osprey, which instead bootstraps drafters from off-the-shelf pretrained small language models, treating broad pretraining as a reusable, target-agnostic asset and reducing per-target work to a lightweight adaptation step. Realizing this requires overcoming two challenges: small LMs are far deeper than a latency-bound drafter can afford, and their pretrained computation must remain intact while the drafter learns to ingest target hidden states and emit tokens in the target's vocabulary. Osprey addresses both by pruning to a shallow backbone, restoring its language-modeling capability with target-agnostic next-token pretraining, and adapting it to each target through vocabulary alignment, zero-initialized QKV expansion, and distillation from the target model's output distribution. Empirically, a single pretrained Osprey backbone transfers across targets and improves mean acceptance length by 16.1% for Qwen3-8B, 21.2% for Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct, and 22.7% for the 229B MiniMax-M2.5 (with 17.5% higher tokens per second), with the largest gains on out-of-domain and multilingual data. Our code is available at https://github.com/LeanModels/Osprey.

</details>


### [5] [The Answer Path and the Grounding Instruction in LLM Question Answering over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10237)
*Arquimedes Canedo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了图检索增强生成（Graph RAG）流水线中四个设计选择的影响：三元组选择、语法、顺序和 grounding 指令。在六个大语言模型和两个知识图谱问答基准上实验发现：包含答案路径（关键三元组链）至关重要，移除它会损失大部分图增益；检索预算应投入召回率，精度在可测试范围内无增益；grounding 指令影响极大，无上下文时强制仅用提供事实会使 F1 从 0.299 跌至 0.035；语法、三元组顺序和子图大小在多跳深度下无可测量影响。作者还发现并撤回了一个因实验设计缺陷导致的虚假结论（graph context hurts at depth）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Graph RAG 流水线涉及多个设计决策（选哪些三元组、用什么语法、什么顺序、什么指令），但以往工作缺乏系统性的消融实验来量化各因素的独立贡献。本文旨在填补这一空白，弄清哪些选择真正影响性能，哪些无关紧要，并纠正文献中可能存在的虚假发现。

Method: 在六个大语言模型和两个知识图谱问答基准上，控制变量法系统变换四个因素：1) 三元组选择：是否包含答案路径、无关实体替换、召回与精度权衡；2) grounding 指令：有无上下文时是否强制仅用提供事实；3) 语法：不同三元组序列化格式；4) 三元组顺序与子图大小。子图来自金标准 SPARQL 查询，排除检索器噪声。使用 F1 作为评价指标。

Result: 1) 答案路径是关键：固定三元组数量时，用无关实体替换非路径三元组仅改变 +0.003 F1，移除路径则损失大部分图增益。检索预算应分配给召回，精度无增益。2) grounding 指令影响巨大：无上下文时加指令使 F1 从 0.299 跌至 0.035（8.63 倍下降）。仅在上下文臂加指令会制造“图上下文在深度有害”的虚假现象，作者发现并撤回了该结论。3) 语法、三元组顺序、子图大小在多跳深度下无可测量效应。4) 因格式敏感评分器，grounding 指令与正确上下文的直接对比无法量化，作为开放对比报告。

Conclusion: Graph RAG 的核心价值在于提供答案路径三元组，其他设计选择（语法、顺序、子图大小）在多跳任务中影响可忽略。检索策略应优先保证召回覆盖关键路径。grounding 指令是双刃剑：它能强制模型依赖上下文，但若上下文为空则灾难性降低性能；实验设计必须同时对基线和实验组应用相同指令，否则会产生误导性结论。未来工作需解决格式敏感评分器带来的评估困境，并探索更鲁棒的指令设计。

Abstract: A graph retrieval-augmented generation pipeline chooses which triples to put in the prompt, a syntax to write them in, an order to write them in, and a sentence telling the model what to do with them. We vary all four over six large language models and two knowledge-graph question answering benchmarks. Two of the four choices move the answer and the other two are flat. The first is whether the answer path, the triples needed to reach the answer, is in the prompt at all. Holding the number of triples fixed and replacing every triple that is not on the chain with material from an unrelated entity changes answer accuracy by +0.003 F1, while removing the chain costs most of what the graph was worth. Retrieval budget belongs on recall, and precision in the range we can test buys nothing. There is no retriever here: subgraphs come from gold SPARQL, so precision describes the context we build, not a system setting. The second is the grounding instruction. With no facts in the prompt, telling a model to answer using only the provided facts drops F1 from 0.299 to 0.035, a factor of 8.63. That figure describes an evaluation with an empty context arm rather than a working pipeline, and an experiment that applies the instruction to its context arm but not to its no-context baseline manufactures a spurious finding that graph context hurts at depth. We found one in our own results and retract it. Syntax, triple order and subgraph size produce no effect we can measure at multi-hop depth. The comparison that would price the grounding instruction against correct context is not measurable with a format-sensitive scorer, because the instruction determines the response format; we report it as an open contrast rather than a number.

</details>


### [6] [SWORD: Wikidata-based Distortions Reveal Hidden Cross-Lingual Inconsistencies in LLM Factual Error Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09349)
*Sanghyeok Park,Minji Kang,Hosung Kwak,Jinhyuk Yun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 SWORD 基准，通过扰动 Wikidata 三元组生成多语言事实错误陈述，揭示大语言模型在多语言事实推理中存在非对称能力：模型在语义合理的扭曲上表现反而优于随机替换，且在东亚语言上性能显著下降，跨语言差距高达 28 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言基准主要奖励选择正确答案，而非评估真实的事实理解能力，无法揭示模型是否真正验证事实还是仅依赖分布熟悉度。

Method: 基于 Wikidata 三元组进行受控扰动，生成八种广泛使用语言中句法良好但事实错误的陈述，扰动类型从随机实体替换到语义合理的属性选择。

Result: 1) 模型在语义合理扭曲上的准确率反而高于随机替换，显示依赖分布熟悉度而非事实验证。2) 基线准确率相当的模型在东亚语言的扭曲陈述上性能大幅下降，跨语言差距达 28 百分点（相对减少 49%）。

Conclusion: 多语言事实推理涉及非对称能力，聚合准确率指标系统性地掩盖了这一问题，需采用扭曲基准进行更细粒度的评估。

Abstract: Modern LLMs demonstrate impressive multilingual performance, yet standard benchmarks primarily reward selecting correct answers rather than evaluating genuine factual understanding. We introduce Systematic Wikidata-based Object-Relation Distortion (SWORD), a benchmark that evaluates whether models consistently reject factual errors across languages. SWORD generates syntactically well-formed but factually incorrect statements in eight widely spoken languages through controlled perturbations of Wikidata triples, ranging from random entity substitutions to semantically plausible property-based selections. Our distortion-based evaluation surfaces two critical insights that remain entirely obscured by conventional benchmarks. First, models counterintuitively achieve higher accuracy on semantically plausible distortions than on nonsensical random substitutions, suggesting reliance on distributional familiarity rather than genuine factual verification. Second, models exhibiting comparable baseline accuracy across languages show substantial performance degradation specifically on (East) Asian languages when presented with distorted statements, with cross-lingual performance gaps reaching up to 28 percentage points (49\% relative reduction) in some models. These findings demonstrate that multilingual factual reasoning involves asymmetric capabilities that aggregate accuracy metrics systematically obscure.

</details>


### [7] [GANDR: Claim Auditing for Verifiable Legal Answer Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10293)
*Chen Qian,Yimeng Wang,Yu Chen,Lingfei Wu,Andreas Stathopoulos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 GANDR，一个双代理系统（起草者+评论者），用于法律问答中的逐条引用验证。起草者按结构化法律推理格式生成回答，评论者像人工验证者一样逐条审核每个引用，并输出逐条审计轨迹。实验在 185 项法律基准上显示，GANDR 在所有主要指标上均排名第一，严格准确率达 70.8%，领先最强基线 11.3 个百分点（p<0.01）。消融实验表明协议锚定提交规则贡献 22.7 点提升，且优势在多个骨干模型上均保持。审计模块作为二分检测器对支撑不足的声明 F1 达 0.84，四分类裁决标签与人工一致性较弱，仅供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有落地生成管道对整体回答打分，导致正确结论可能依赖捏造或松散匹配的引用却仍获高分。高风险领域（如法律实务）要求读者能逐条核实每个声明对应的引用来源，因此需要面向逐条验证的系统设计与评测体系。

Method: GANDR 采用双代理架构：(1) 起草器按结构化法律推理格式撰写回答；(2) 评论器拥有与人工验证者相同的视角，逐条审核每个声明引用的源文档，并每轮输出逐条审计轨迹。配合严格正确性准则：每个引用必须解析到检索器返回的文本片段。

Result: 在共享同一骨干模型、检索表面和引用指令的 185 项法律基准上，GANDR 在所有主要指标上均居首位，严格准确率 70.8%，比最强基线高 11.3 点（p<0.01）。移除协议锚定提交规则会使严格准确率下降 22.7 点；在另外三个骨干模型上仍保持 +3.2 到 +6.5 点的优势。优势主要源于起草器配置与协议锚定提交，而非重写机制。审计模块作为二分检测器对支撑不足声明的 F1 为 0.84，四分类裁决标签与两位法学标注员一致性较弱，仅具参考价值。

Conclusion: GANDR 通过结构化起草与逐条审计相结合，显著提升了法律问答中引用的可验证性与严格准确率。协议锚定提交规则是关键设计点。审计模块可作为高可靠的二分支撑检测器，但细粒度裁决仍需人工介入。代码将按需公开。

Abstract: In high-stakes domains such as legal practice, a language-model answer is only useful to the extent that a reader can verify each claim against the source the system cites. Current grounded-generation pipelines score the answer as a whole, so a correct conclusion can rest on fabricated or loosely matched citations and still score well. Closing this gap requires both a system built for per-claim verification and an evaluation that measures it. We introduce GANDR (Grounded ANswer DRafter), a two-agent system in which a Drafter writes an answer in a structured legal-reasoning format and a separate Critic, with the same view as a human verifier, audits each claim against its cited source and emits a per-claim audit trace on every round. We pair it with a strict correctness criterion requiring every citation to resolve to a passage the retriever returned. On a 185-item legal benchmark where all six systems share one backbone, one retrieval surface, and one citation instruction, GANDR ranks first on every primary metric, reaching 70.8% strict accuracy and leading the strongest baseline by 11.3 points (p<0.01). Reverting the protocol-anchored commit rule lowers strict accuracy by 22.7 points, and the strict lead stays positive on three further backbones, at +3.2 to +6.5 points. This lead traces to the Drafter configuration and the protocol-anchored commit, not to rewriting. Against two law-trained annotators the audit flags under-supported claims at F1 0.84 as a binary detector, while its four-way verdict labels agree only weakly and are advisory. Code is available upon request.

</details>


### [8] [Auditable Emergency Triage for Maternal and Newborn Care in India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09356)
*Shobhit Jagga,Aman Dalmia,Niharika Priyadarshini,Neelima Devadas,Amrita K Prasen,Nikhil Nalin,Santhosh SJ,Sreeram Nurani Ramasubramanian,Muhammed Afeer K,Anubhav Arora*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Noora Health 将基于 LLM 的紧急分诊系统从不透明的单步推理重构为两阶段管道：LLM 提取标准化症状与患者背景，确定性规则引擎判定急症。此举显著提升召回率（0.565→0.810）和 F1（0.606→0.702），并实现可审计性、低成本规则迭代，部署后处理 15 万+ 查询，过度升级率 17.8% 且无漏报增加，临床医生已新增 48 条规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原系统采用扁平危险征兆列表且依赖 LLM 单步推理，导致不透明、错误分析困难、提示词修改需全量回归测试成本高昂，且临床决策树未文档化传递给模型。

Method: 将分诊分解为两步：1) LLM 使用临床医生编写的词表从查询中抽取规范症状与患者背景；2) 确定性规则引擎基于结构化规则判定是否为急症。规则由临床专家编写、可独立新增、无需重跑评估。

Result: 召回率从 0.565 提升至 0.810，F1 从 0.606 提升至 0.702；部署后处理 152,421 条查询，标记 18.7% 为急症，过度升级率 17.8%，未增加漏报；临床医生部署后新增 48 条规则，验证了快速修正闭环。

Conclusion: 结构化症状抽取 + 确定性规则引擎的分解式设计，在保持 LLM 语义理解能力的同时，获得了可审计性、低成本迭代与临床可控性，显著提升分诊性能并支撑大规模生产部署。

Abstract: At Noora Health, our nurses answer more than 50,000 medical queries per month on our WhatsApp-based service that provides caregivers with on-demand support. Their most time-critical task is emergency triage: deciding which queries need immediate in-person attention. To support them, we built a system that uses a large language model (LLM) to classify whether a message is an emergency and provide a rationale for interpretability. But the system was opaque: analyzing mistakes meant reading reasoning chains for each message, which is infeasible at our scale. Prompt changes meant re-running a full evaluation to prevent regressions, which was both costly and operationally challenging. Clinicians follow a decision tree to make this call, but it was never documented or passed to the model, which relied on a flat list of danger signs. To address these issues, we decomposed triage into two steps: an LLM extracts canonical symptoms and patient context from the query using a clinician-authored vocabulary, and a deterministic rule engine captures the scenarios that indicate an emergency. We show that the new system raised recall from 0.565 to 0.810 and F1 from 0.606 to 0.702, with structured rules driving most of the accuracy gains while the decomposition provides auditability: clinical experts can inspect each stage of the new system to see whether the query was mistranslated, symptoms were incorrectly extracted, patient context was wrongly inferred, or the necessary rules were missing. They can add new rules independently without causing regressions and avoid running costly evaluations. Since deployment, the new system has triaged 152,421 patient queries and flagged 28,535 (18.7%) as emergencies. The over-escalation rate has been 17.8%, without any increase in missed emergencies. Clinicians have also added 48 new rules since deployment, evidence of the faster correction loop we set out to build.

</details>


### [9] [Do LLMs Make More Mistakes If They Do Not Believe the Input Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09363)
*Peter Kochelka,Aleš Manuel Papáček,Vojtěch Dvořák,Ondřej Dušek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型（LLM）在检索增强生成和数据到文本系统中的忠实度问题，重点分析上下文可信度（context-memory conflict）对生成忠实度的影响。通过让模型用英语、捷克语、斯洛伐克语和上索布语从事实性（FA）、反事实性（CFA）和虚构（FI）RDF三元组生成文本，发现上下文-记忆冲突较弱：反事实输入的忠实度评分仅比事实输入低0.05分（1-5分制）。同时指出，若选择不当的LLM裁判会高估冲突强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型容易产生幻觉或误解事实，降低其在检索增强生成（RAG）和数据到文本系统中的可用性。需理解模型对提供上下文的忠实度如何受其感知的上下文合理性（即上下文与参数化记忆的冲突）影响。

Method: 利用非英语及低资源语言生成难度较高、且基于模型参数知识仅部分覆盖的本地知识作为输入。构建包含捷克和斯洛伐克本地数据的事实性（FA）、反事实性（CFA）、虚构（FI）RDF三元组，让模型用英语、捷克语、斯洛伐克语和上索布语生成文本。采用人工标注样本评估，并用Kimi K3作为LLM裁判与人工标注对比。

Result: 在人工标注样本上仅观测到弱上下文-记忆冲突。Kimi K3作为LLM裁判与人工标注一致性良好，反事实输入的忠实度评分仅比事实输入低0.05（1-5分制）。若选用次优LLM裁判，会高估上下文-记忆冲突的强度。

Conclusion: 上下文可信度对LLM生成忠实度的影响较小，反事实上下文仅轻微降低忠实度。评估时需谨慎选择LLM裁判，避免高估冲突效应。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are prone to hallucinating or misinterpreting facts, which impairs their usability in retrieval-augmented generation or data-to-text systems. We analyse how faithfulness of LLMs to provided context depends on how plausible they perceive the context to be (context-memory conflict). To better identify error patterns, we make use of the increased difficulty of non-English and low-resource language text generation and input data based on local knowledge, only partially captured in models' parametric knowledge. We let the models generate text in English, Czech, Slovak and Upper Sorbian from factual (FA), counterfactual (CFA) and fictional (FI) RDF triples containing local Czech and Slovak data. Contrary to our expectations, we observe only a weak context-memory conflict on the human-annotated sample. For Kimi K3 as an LLM judge, which agrees well with human annotations on the sample, counterfactual inputs receive only slightly lower faithfulness scores than factual ones (-0.05 on a 1-5 scale). We also find that a suboptimal choice of LLM judge would lead to overestimating the strength of the context-memory conflict.

</details>


### [10] [Benchmarking Hybrid Deep Research Across Database Querying and Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09410)
*Ruofan Wu,Peiran Xu,Xiaolong Li,Fan Shu,Soyoung Yoon,Yite Wang,Xiaodong Yu,Boyi Liu,Feng Yan,Debiao Li,Yuxiong He,Zhewei Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 HybridDeepResearch，首个要求结合网络搜索与 SQL 查询的深度研究基准，包含 380 个工具依赖任务，评估显示即使最先进模型在困难子集上也仅达 50-54% Pass@8，揭示了跨模态约束保持的重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究基准仅评估单一环境（如纯网络搜索或纯结构化数据库），忽略了现实复杂任务需在非结构化文本与结构化数据间穿梭、保持约束的“交接”能力。

Method: 构建基于 LiveSQLBench-Base-Lite 数据库与公开网络语料的 380 个任务，涵盖 SQL2S、S2SQL、Parallel 三种推理模式，通过自动化检查与人工审核验证，在多种 Agent 框架下评估专有与开源模型。

Result: SOTA 模型（GLM-5.2、Claude-Sonnet-4.6、GPT-5）在困难子集上仅达 50-54% Pass@8；定向推理（SQL2S、S2SQL）显著难于并行交集，表明跨模态约束保持仍是核心难点。

Conclusion: HybridDeepResearch 揭示了当前 Agent 系统在融合结构化与非结构化信息空间时丢失约束的根本缺陷，为未来跨模态深度研究提供了关键评测平台。

Abstract: While autonomous agents have made significant strides in "deep research" by iteratively navigating the open web to synthesize information, real-world problem-solving is rarely confined to a single environment. Complex analytical tasks inherently require agents to weave together evidence from both ambiguous unstructured text (e.g., the open web) and highly precise structured data (e.g., relational databases). However, existing benchmarks evaluate these modalities in isolation, failing to capture the critical "handoff" - the ability to preserve constraints when moving evidence between systems. We introduce HybridDeepResearch, to our knowledge the first deep-research benchmark that requires both web search and SQL to form a complete, verifiable answer. The benchmark contains 380 tool-dependent tasks grounded in LiveSQLBench-Base-Lite databases and public web corpora, validated through automated checks and human review, and covering three reasoning patterns: SQL2S, S2SQL, and Parallel. Evaluations across proprietary and open-weight models under various agentic scaffolds reveal that even state-of-the-art models like GLM-5.2, Claude-Sonnet-4.6 and GPT-5 achieve only about 50-54% Pass@8 on the hard subset. Notably, results show that directional reasoning is substantially more difficult than parallel intersection, highlighting that bridging structured and unstructured information spaces without losing constraints remains a major open challenge for agentic systems. Code and datasets are publicly available at GitHub (https://github.com/Snowflake-AI-Research/HybridDeepResearch) and Hugging Face (https://huggingface.co/datasets/Snowflake/HybridDeepResearch).

</details>


### [11] [Edu-QuRating: Multi-Dimensional Educational Data Curation with Distilled Pairwise Judgements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09425)
*Oliver G. B. Garrod,Robin A. A. Ince,Meng Liu,Mohamed Huti,Moritz Boos,Amy Waldock,Dominic Andrews,Paul Atherton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Edu-QuRating，一种多维度的教育数据评分与筛选管道，用于改进语言模型的预训练和后训练。该方法定义教育专用评分标准，利用 LLM 判断器标注文档对偏好，并蒸馏成可复用的 Edu-QuRater 模型，对文本块在多个教育维度打分。实验表明，Edu-QuRater 在六项教育标准上能以 0.917 平均准确率恢复 GPT-4.1-mini 的成对判断。应用于 3.22 亿 FineWeb-Edu-Fortified 文档的语料筛选，基于 Edu-QuRating 的预训练混合数据在九项基准上显著优于 FineWeb-Edu 基线；结合 GRPO 后训练奖励，生成的回答在教学质量和指令遵循上均优于 Qwen3-4B 基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育数据过滤器通常将教育价值视为单一标量，过于粗糙，特别是在数据集已具有高密度教育材料时。有用的学习材料需要准确、引人入胜、结构良好，并针对目标受众（如学习者与教师）适配。因此需要一种多维度、细粒度的教育数据评分与筛选方法。

Method: Edu-QuRating 管道包含三步：(1) 定义教育专用评分细则；(2) 使用 LLM 判断器对采样的文档对进行成对偏好标注；(3) 将成对偏好蒸馏为可复用的 Edu-QuRater 序列分类模型，可对单个文本块在多个教育维度（如准确性、吸引力、结构性、受众适配性等）打分。实验使用两种序列分类基座模型和六项教育标准，并在 GPT-4.1-mini 成对判断上验证。随后将评分器应用于语料筛选（322.25M 文档）和 GRPO 后训练奖励建模。

Result: 最佳 Edu-QuRater 在六项教育标准上恢复 GPT-4.1-mini 成对判断的平均准确率为 0.917。在预训练实验中，基于 Edu-QuRating 筛选的混合数据训练的模型在九项基准上的聚合准确率高于 FineWeb-Edu 基线，提升集中在特定任务。在 GRPO 后训练中，结合 Edu-QuRater 奖励和答案结构奖励，生成的回答在教学质量和指令遵循的成对评估中均优于 Qwen3-4B 基座模型。

Conclusion: Edu-QuRating 提供了一种实用的多维度教育数据评分与筛选框架，能够捕捉教育材料的细微质量差异。该方法不仅显著提升了小模型的预训练效果，还通过奖励建模增强了模型的教学能力和指令遵循能力，为教育场景下的语言模型训练提供了更精细的数据质量控制手段。

Abstract: Educational data filters have become a practical way to improve language-model pre-training, but most filters treat educational value as a single scalar property. This may be too broad for some applications, especially if the data set already features a high density of educational material. Useful learning material needs to be accurate, engaging, well structured, and appropriate for the intended audience and application (e.g. learner- vs teacher-facing). Following QuRating (Wettig et al. 2024), we introduce Edu-QuRating: a pipeline for multi-dimensional educational data scoring and curation. Edu-QuRating defines education-specific rubrics, uses an LLM judge to label sampled document pairs and distills those pairwise preferences into reusable Edu-QuRaters, which can score individual text chunks on a set of educational criteria. Across two sequence-classification base models and six educational criteria, the best Edu-QuRater recovers held-out GPT-4.1-mini pairwise judgements with mean accuracy 0.917. We then apply the resulting scorers in two applications. First, we investigate the potential of Edu-QuRaters for corpus filtering to improve pretraining of small language models. We scored 322.25M FineWeb-Edu-Fortified documents to obtain a filtered pre-training mixture. In matched single-run pre-training comparisons, models trained with Edu-QuRating-based mixtures reached higher observed aggregate accuracy across nine benchmarks than the FineWeb-Edu baseline, with gains concentrated in particular tasks. Second, we used Edu-QuRater scores as reward terms for GRPO post-training. In held-out pairwise judge evaluations, combining Edu-QuRater and answer-structure rewards produced responses preferred to the Qwen3-4B base model on both pedagogical quality and instruction following.

</details>


### [12] [The Mutations of Machine Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09496)
*Mauricio Figueroa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文追踪了算法输出的演变，识别出机器言语的三重变异：搜索引擎将言语重构为可查询数据、社交媒体将言语重构为参与度指标、生成式对话系统取代信息检索并带来深层技术法律纠缠。文章旨在系统梳理算法言语辩论，阐明法律在技术变革中的构成性作用，为跨学科研究者提供概念资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法输出已渗透组织当代生活的数字环境，法律不再仅是回应技术，更参与塑造技术演进。现有学术讨论碎片化，亟需系统性框架将技术变迁与法律的构成性角色关联起来，以便研究者、政策制定者等应对不断演变的算法治理挑战。

Method: 采用谱系学式的概念史分析，梳理算法输出演变的三个阶段（搜索引擎、社交媒体、生成式系统），揭示每阶段言语本体论的重构及其法律底层逻辑，综合表达自由、信息隐私、传播研究等领域的碎片化文献，构建跨学科的分析框架。

Result: 识别出机器言语的三重变异：第一，搜索引擎将网络从信息检索空间转化为算法可见性经济政权；第二，平台融合内容审核与算法放大，将表达转化为注意力度量；第三，生成式系统以合成文本取代检索，产生密集的技术法律纠缠与认识论后果。法律在这些变异中扮演构成性而非仅仅是反应性角色。

Conclusion: 算法言语辩论的核心在于法律如何参与构建技术基础设施的本体论预设。本文提供的概念框架有助于超越技术决定论与法律滞后论的二元对立，为理解和干预算法治理中的权力分配、认知秩序重塑提供分析工具，呼吁法律学术更主动地介入技术架构的规范性设计。

Abstract: Algorithmic outputs now populate the digital environments through which contemporary life is organized. The role of law in facilitating and constituting (rather than merely responding to) these processes is gaining increasing traction across scholarly accounts. This inquiry traces the evolution of algorithmic outputs attending to their legal underpinnings and social implications, surfacing the mutations of machine speech.
  The first mutation redefined speech as data to be queried: search engines transformed the web from a space of information retrieval into an economic regime of algorithmic visibility. The second mutation reframed speech as engagement: social media platforms fused moderation with amplification, turning expression into a metric of attention, governed by corporate architectures. The third mutation emerges in conversational systems and interfaces, where generative text displaces information retrieval, bringing with it dense technolegal entanglements and profound epistemic consequences.
  Scholars of freedom of expression, informational privacy, and communication studies have long grappled with these dynamics, yet their implications for broader legal thought have also become urgent. This piece seeks to organize and clarify the evolving debate around algorithmic speech, making this critical but often fragmented discourse more accessible to wider legal and interdisciplinary audiences. In doing so, it bridges the gap between observing technological transformation and critically assessing the constitutive role of law within it, offering a conceptual resource for researchers, students, policymakers, and practitioners navigating and contesting this evolving landscape.

</details>


### [13] [TEFM: Token-Efficient Faithful Modeling for Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09552)
*Zhichao Hou,Lingdao Sha,Xueyu Mao,Yang Liu,Peijie Qiu,Rui Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TEFM（Token-Efficient Faithful Modeling）框架，通过将结构化观测压缩为紧凑的Behavioral Code token实现token效率，并通过双保真目标联合优化代码级重构和预测级保真度实现忠实合理化，在临床和安全领域实现约1%-2%的token保留率的同时保持竞争性分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决将LLMs应用于关键领域时面临的两大根本障碍：token效率（冗长结构化观测消耗过多token）和忠实性（需要基于输入数据的忠实合理化解释）。

Method: TEFM框架包含两大核心组件：1) Token效率：将冗长结构化观测压缩为紧凑的Behavioral Code token，大幅减少token消耗且信息损失极小。2) 忠实合理化：采用双保真目标，联合优化代码级重构保真度和预测级保真度，识别基于输入数据的最小充分特征子集。

Result: 在多个领域数据集和模型骨干（Qwen3、Gemma-2、Phi-4）上的综合实验表明，TEFM在保持竞争性分类准确率的同时实现显著token减少（临床领域约1% token保留率，安全领域约2%），并能产生忠实的合理化解释。

Conclusion: TEFM成功联合解决了关键领域结构化数据分析中的token效率和忠实性约束，为LLMs在资源受限、高风险环境中的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we solve two fundamental obstacles in applying LLMs to critical domains: token efficiency and faithfulness. To address both constraints jointly, we present TEFM (Token-Efficient Faithful Modeling), a framework designed for structured data analysis in critical domains. TEFM achieves token efficiency by compressing lengthy structured observations into compact Behavioral Code tokens, dramatically reducing token consumption with minimal information loss. Moreover, TEFM enables faithful rationalization through a dual-fidelity objective that jointly optimizes code-level reconstruction and prediction-level fidelity, identifying minimal sufficient feature subsets grounded in input data. Comprehensive experiments across various domain datasets and model backbones (Qwen3, Gemma-2, Phi-4) show that TEFM achieves competitive classification accuracy with dramatic token reduction (approximately 1\% token retention in clinical and 2\% in security domains) while producing faithful rationales.

</details>


### [14] [BuzzASR: A Swarm of 100+ Monolingual Speech Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09554)
*Shivam Singh,Aditya Yadavalli,Catherine Arnett,Alex Warstadt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 BuzzASR，针对 102 种语言对 Whisper 进行语言专用微调，结合单语分词器替换和纯文本微调增强，在 FLEURS 和 Common Voice 测试集上大幅降低 CER，超越 Whisper-large-v3 并达到多语言开源 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper 等大规模多语言端到端 ASR 模型在低资源语言上表现不佳。虽然单语微调被证实有效，但此前仅应用于少数语言，缺乏大规模系统性探索。

Method: 1. 在 FLEURS 覆盖的 102 种语言上对 Whisper-large-v3 进行单语监督微调；2. 引入单语分词器替换策略，用语言专用 BPE 替换原多语言 BPE；3. 利用纯文本数据进行文本仅微调作为数据增强；4. 在 FLEURS 与 Common Voice 测试集上全面评估。

Result: BuzzASR 在 102 种语言中 77 种超越 Whisper-large-v3，平均 CER 降低 2.8 倍以上；在 27 种语言上达到开源系统 SOTA；分词器替换使压缩率平均提升 3.3 倍，最高 21.7 倍。

Conclusion: 大规模单语微调配合分词器本地化能显著提升低资源语言 ASR 性能，且纯文本微调可进一步增强模型。开源的模型、代码与详细结果为多语言 ASR 研究提供重要基准。

Abstract: We introduce BuzzASR, a collection of language-specialized fine-tuned Whisper models adapted for automatic speech recognition (ASR) in 102 languages. Large end-to-end Transformer-based ASR models such as Whisper have revolutionized ASR, but most prominent models are highly multilingual. As a result, these models often perform poorly on languages less well-represented in their training set. While it has long been known that effective language adaptation can be achieved through simple fine-tuning on monolingual data, this strategy has only been applied to a small number of languages. We massively scale up this simple approach to 102 languages covered in the FLEURS dataset, while also implementing a more complex language adaptation strategy that integrates monolingual tokenizer replacement and data augmentation using text-only fine-tuning. BuzzASR models outperform Whisper-large-v3 on 77 out of 102 languages, reducing character error rates (CER) by a factor of over 2.8 on average. Our models achieve state-of-the-art CER among open-source systems on 27 of 102 languages on the combined FLEURS and Common Voice test set. Our tokenizer replacement strategy yields an average 3.3x improvement in compression rate (characters per token) over Whisper's multilingual BPE, with gains of up to 21.7x. We release all models, code, and detailed results: https://lemn-lab.github.io/buzz-asr

</details>


### [15] [Towards Automatic Evolution Tree Generation from Citation Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09561)
*Zexing Zhao,Yuntong Hu,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 EvoTree 分阶段框架，解耦概念骨干学习与时间细化，构建 AI 子领域方法谱系分类树，发布首个 11 子领域标注基准，实验显示 EvoTree 在 NMI、引用方向准确性、概念纯度等指标上均优于基线，且为唯一能非平凡检测边缘论文的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述难以跟上论文发布速度；现有分类诱导方法多为叶子节点绑定且忽略时间，倾向将过渡性论文强行归入成熟叶子，导致祖先后代拓扑倒置。

Method: EvoTree 分阶段框架：1) 图感知编码器结合基于分布的层次聚类生成稳定分类骨干；2) 时间微调在单调路径约束下将边缘论文重新连接到内部节点；3) 最终 LLM 标签概念而不改变拓扑。发布 11 个 AI 子领域标注基准。

Result: EvoTree 在所有基线中达到最高 NMI 和引用方向准确性，在标注基准上获得最佳概念纯度，且是唯一在标注集上实现非平凡边缘论文检测的方法。

Conclusion: EvoTree 有效解决了 AI 子领域方法谱系构建中的扩展性、时间感知和边缘论文处理问题，提供了新基准和最优性能。

Abstract: Surveys remain the primary way researchers grasp the lineage of methods within an AI subfield, but they scale poorly against the current rate of publication. Existing taxonomy-induction methods are largely leaf-bound and time-agnostic; they tend to force transitional papers into mature leaves and can create topological inversions between ancestors and descendants. We propose EvoTree, a staged framework that decouples conceptual backbone learning from temporal refinement: a graph-aware encoder with distribution-based hierarchical clustering yields a stable taxonomy backbone; temporal fine-tuning then re-attaches marginal papers to internal nodes under monotonic-path constraints; a final LLM pass labels concepts without altering the topology. We release the first annotated benchmark for this task across 11 AI subfields. EvoTree attains the highest NMI and citation-direction accuracy among all baselines and the best concept purity on the annotated benchmark, and is the only method with non-trivial marginal-paper detection on the annotated set.

</details>


### [16] [Reproducing Omitted Temporal Expressions in Japanese News for Retrieval-Augmented Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09569)
*Tomoaki Yasuda,Shotaro Ishihara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出jaROTE，一种基于规则的管道，用于利用发布日期还原日语新闻文章中省略的时间表达，实验表明其性能高、成本低，且能改善时限词汇检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻文章常包含省略的时间表达（如仅日期或仅月份），需结合发布日期理解。若作为独立文本被索引或用于搜索和RAG系统，这些省略会导致时间错配和大语言模型理解不稳定。

Method: 基于成熟的时间表达抽取与归一化技术，并结合日语新闻文章的人工分析，提出jaROTE：一个面向日语新闻的基于规则的管道，利用发布日期作为外部上下文还原省略的时间表达为具体日期或区间。

Result: 在两个新闻语料库上的实验显示，jaROTE达到了高性能，与大语言模型竞争力相当，同时具备快速、低成本的优势。进一步证明时间还原能提升时限词汇检索效果，验证了基于发布日期归一化的实际价值。

Conclusion: 基于发布日期的时间归一化对于日语新闻检索具有实用价值，jaROTE提供了一种高效、低成本的解决方案，优于或相当于LLM方法。

Abstract: News articles often contain omitted temporal expressions, such as day-only or month-only mentions, which must be interpreted with reference to the publication date. When such articles are indexed or processed as standalone text in search and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems, these omissions can cause temporal mismatches and unstable interpretation by large language models. We focus on reproducing omitted temporal expressions as concrete dates or intervals using the publication date as external context before the articles are indexed for search and RAG applications. Specifically, building on established temporal-expression extraction and normalization techniques and informed by a manual analysis of Japanese news articles, we propose jaROTE, a rule-based pipeline for Japanese news. Experiments on two news corpora demonstrate that jaROTE achieves high performance, and remains competitive with LLMs while providing a fast, low-cost pipeline. We further show that temporal reproduction improves time-constrained lexical retrieval, demonstrating the practical value of publication-date-grounded normalization for Japanese news retrieval.

</details>


### [17] [Beyond Top Words: MonoTM for Topic Modeling with Interpretable Monosemantic Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09575)
*Una Joh,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MonoTM 是一种可解释的主题建模框架，利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）将文档-主题混合估计与语义解释解耦：用完整的 SAE 特征袋表示估计混合分布，固定混合后再基于语料库锚定的语义特征词表学习主题描述符，从而在保留全局主题结构的同时，用比单词更有意义的语义单元表示主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主题模型依赖高频词描述主题，难以充分捕捉主题语义；稀疏自编码器虽能从稠密表示中提取可解释特征，但特征可解释性与主题推断质量的关系尚不明确。

Method: MonoTM 将任务分为两阶段：第一阶段使用完整的 SAE 特征袋表示估计文档-主题混合分布；第二阶段固定混合分布，在一个独立的、基于语料库的语义特征词表上学习主题描述符，从而实现混合估计与语义解释的解耦。

Result: 在三个基准语料库上实验表明，文档-主题混合估计与语义解释偏好不同的 SAE 配置和特征子集；MonoTM 能同时保持全局主题结构，并生成比单词更具语义意义的主题表示。

Conclusion: 解耦混合估计与语义解释的设计使主题表示更有利于下游语料库分析任务，证明了在主题建模中分离这两个角色的有效性。

Abstract: Topic models summarize large text corpora, but top-ranked words often provide only a limited representation of topic semantics. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) offer a way to move beyond word-level descriptors by extracting interpretable features from dense representations, yet how feature interpretability relates to topic-inference quality remains unclear. We introduce \textbf{MonoTM}, an interpretable topic modeling framework that decouples these roles. Across three benchmark corpora, we show that document--topic mixture estimation and semantic interpretation favor different SAE configurations and feature subsets. MonoTM estimates mixtures from the full SAE bag-of-features representation and, with them fixed, learns topic descriptors over a separate vocabulary of corpus-grounded semantic features. This design preserves global topic structure while representing topics with semantic units more meaningful than individual words, making them more useful for downstream corpus analysis.

</details>


### [18] [SEA-SpeechBench: A Large-Scale Multitask Benchmark for Speech Understanding Across Southeast Asia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09672)
*Jingyi Liao,Wenyu Zhang,Zhuohan Liu,Yingxu He,Geyu Lin,Xunlong Zou,Shuo Sun,Syed Ali Redha Alsagoff,Ai Ti Aw*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEA-SpeechBench 是首个大规模多任务基准，用于评估 11 种东南亚语言的语音理解能力，包含 97,194 个样本、99 个评测集、597 小时音频，覆盖 9 项任务（语音处理、副语言分析、时序理解）。评测显示主流模型在时序理解、情感识别、语音翻译及低资源语言（如缅甸语、泰米尔语）提示词表现上存在显著短板，英语提示词领先高达 41 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语音及多模态大语言模型评测框架以英语为中心，东南亚语言严重缺乏代表性，亟需包容性的多语言评测基准以推动模型公平发展。

Method: 构建 SEA-SpeechBench 基准，涵盖 3 大类 9 项任务：语音处理（自动语音识别、语音翻译、口语问答）、副语言分析（情感、性别、年龄、说话人识别）、时序理解（时间戳内容查询、长音频时序定位）。采用母语及英语双语提示词，评测主流开源与专有模型。

Result: 所有模型在时序理解、情感识别、语音翻译任务上表现不佳；低资源语言（缅甸语、泰米尔语）提示词性能落后英语高达 41 个百分点，揭示模型在多语言泛化与时序建模上的关键局限。

Conclusion: 研究揭示现有模型在东南亚语言语音理解上的严重不足，强调包容性模型开发的必要性，并公开发布 SEA-SpeechBench 以促进后续研究。}

Abstract: The rapid advancement of audio and multimodal large language models has unlocked transformative speech understanding capabilities, yet evaluation frameworks remain predominantly English-centric, leaving Southeast Asian (SEA) languages critically underrepresented. We introduce SEA-SpeechBench, to the best of our knowledge, the first large-scale multitask benchmark that evaluates speech understanding in 11 SEA languages through 97,194 samples across 99 evaluation sets and 597 hours of curated audio data. Our benchmark comprises 9 diverse tasks across 3 categories: speech processing (automatic speech recognition, speech translation, spoken question answering), paralinguistic analysis (emotion, gender, age, speaker recognition), and temporal understanding, a novel dimension featuring timestamped content queries and temporal localization within extended audio sequences up to 3 minutes. We implement multilingual prompting in both native SEA languages and English to reflect user interactions with audio-language models. Evaluation of leading open-source and proprietary systems reveals marked performance gaps. Across all models, performance remains underwhelming on temporal understanding, emotion recognition, and speech translation. Prompting in low-resource languages such as Burmese and Tamil lags behind English by up to 41 percentage points. Our findings expose critical model limitations and underscore the need for inclusive model development. The SEA-SpeechBench benchmark is available at https://zwenyu.github.io/SEA-SpeechBench/.

</details>


### [19] [X2-NativeCursor: Native-Token Text Progress Tracking for Incremental-Text Streaming Codec TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09677)
*Zehan Liu,Carl Chen,Rime Wen,Kaiqi Fu,Altman Lin,Shawn Qin,Lights Shi,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 X2-NativeCursor，一种轻量级流式 TTS 进度追踪器，直接从原生语音 token 预测文本光标位置，无需波形解码，实现了极低误差（0.151 字）和极快实时因子（0.018），且不修改上游 TTS 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增量文本流式 TTS 需要在线追踪文本进度以支持高亮同步、打断处理和对话历史更新。现有基于波形的对齐要么需要完整音频，要么在流式阶段引入额外声学处理，延迟高、计算重。

Method: 设计一个不改变 TTS 生成器的轻量观察者：(1) 归一化规划将已发音标签映射回原文跨度；(2) 文本编码器与原生 token 编码器联合送入局部匹配器估计当前标签位置；(3) 单独的输出规则将可修正的位置估计转换为单调不后退的光标。

Result: 在 Qwen3-TTS 上，80 ms 前瞻下平均绝对误差 0.151 中文字符（基线 320 ms 前瞻下 1.253 字），对齐实时因子从 0.3598 降至 0.0180；第二自动对齐参考下仍保持低误差；另训练观察器即可适配 CosyVoice2。

Conclusion: X2-NativeCursor 通过原生 token 级监督实现了高精度、超低延迟、近零开销的流式文本进度追踪，架构解耦使其易于迁移到不同 TTS 主干，为实时交互式语音合成提供了关键基础设施。

Abstract: Incremental-text streaming text-to-speech (TTS) needs online text progress tracking for synchronized highlighting, interruption handling, and dialogue-history updates. Input text arrives before it is spoken, so text arrival alone cannot indicate speech progress. Existing waveform-based alignment requires complete audio or adds acoustic processing during streaming. We propose X2-NativeCursor, a lightweight observer that tracks progress from native speech tokens before waveform decoding without changing the TTS generator. Its normalization plan links spoken labels to their original-text spans. Text and native-token encoders feed a local matcher that estimates the current label position. A separate output rule converts revisable position estimates into a cursor that never moves backward. Mean absolute error against an automatic reference is 0.151 Chinese characters with 80-ms lookahead, versus 1.253 characters with 320-ms lookahead for an online waveform baseline. Alignment real-time factor also decreases from 0.3598 to 0.0180 relative to this baseline. Lower tracking error is retained under a second automatic alignment reference. We evaluate X2-NativeCursor on Qwen3-TTS and validate its adaptation to CosyVoice2 by training a separate observer for each backbone. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/X-Square-Robot/X2Streaming-TTS.

</details>


### [20] [Which Medical Questions Deserve Rationales? Perturbation-Sensitive Selection for Robust QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09684)
*Yuexin Wu,Dayou Yu,Vasile Rus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RMS-RSP 方法，在固定 token 预算下选择哪些已标注问题应获得理由监督，通过在理由 token 处扰动隐藏状态并测量金标签与最佳干扰项边界的变化来评估鲁棒性。在五个医学 QA 数据集上实验表明，该方法在锁定预算下准确率略高于随机选择（60.61% vs 60.08%），仅在 AfriMed-QA 上显著提升；但在答案选项重排序后，鲁棒准确率和语义一致性分别平均提升 1.91 和 2.85 个点，且五个数据集方向一致。全监督训练虽提高宏准确率至 63.74%，但消耗 29-254 倍理由 token 且不一致提升鲁棒性。结论：理由局部边界敏感性可识别出能提升对语义等价格式变化不变性的监督数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学问答数据集通常包含答案标签，但高质量理由稀缺、嘈杂或验证成本高。因此问题从“哪些问题应被标注”转变为“在固定 token 预算下，哪些已标注问题应获得理由监督”。

Method: 提出均方根鲁棒性样本优先级（RMS-RSP）：在离线设置中，候选理由对选择器可见但除非被选中否则不参与下游训练；仅在理由 token 处扰动隐藏状态，测量金标签与最佳干扰项边界的位移，以此衡量样本的鲁棒性并优先选择。

Result: 跨五个医学 QA 数据集、MedGemma-4B-IT、三个训练种子、十个预算非 RSP 选择器及全监督基准：锁定预算平均准确率 60.61% vs 随机 60.08%，仅 AfriMed-QA 显著提升 +1.44；全预算准确率面积不优于随机。经三次答案选项重排序后，RMS-RSP 平均鲁棒准确率和语义一致性分别提升 1.91 和 2.85 点，五个数据集方向一致。全池理由训练宏准确率达 63.74%，但消耗 29-254 倍理由 token 且不一致改善鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果未确立普遍的准确率提升；而是表明理由局部边界敏感性可识别出能改善模型对语义等价格式变化不变性的监督数据。

Abstract: Medical question-answering datasets often contain answer labels, whereas high-quality rationales remain scarce, noisy, or costly to validate. This changes the acquisition question: rather than asking which questions should be labeled, we ask which already-labeled questions should receive rationale supervision under a fixed token budget. We study an offline version of this problem in which candidate rationales are visible to the selector but withheld from downstream training unless selected. We propose root-mean-square Robustness-based Sample Prioritization (RMS-RSP), which perturbs hidden states only at rationale tokens and measures the resulting shift in the gold-versus-best-distractor margin. Across five medical QA datasets, MedGemma-4B-IT, three training seeds, ten budgeted non-RSP selectors, and an unbudgeted full-supervision reference, RMS-RSP provides a deliberately qualified result. Its locked-budget accuracy is 60.61% on average versus 60.08% for Random, with a statistically resolved gain only on AfriMed-QA (+1.44 points). Its full-budget accuracy area is not better than Random. However, after three answer-option reorderings, RMS-RSP improves robust accuracy and semantic consistency by 1.91 and 2.85 points on average, respectively, with the same direction on all five datasets. Training on every pool rationale raises macro accuracy to 63.74%, but consumes 29--254 times more rationale tokens and does not uniformly improve robustness. These findings do not establish universal accuracy gains; they instead suggest that rationale-local boundary sensitivity can identify supervision that improves invariance to semantically equivalent formatting changes.

</details>


### [21] [Looped GPT-BERT: Trading Parameters for Computation in Small Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09691)
*Tingshuo Fan,Hongtao Mu,Tianyu Zhou,Hansen Liu,Tao Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在BabyLM 2026 Strict-small设置下研究Looped GPT-BERT，通过深度参数共享（循环遍历）实现仅12.18M参数的模型在语言建模任务上达到与更大基线模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在训练数据受限的情况下，增加参数量并非提升语言模型性能的唯一途径；少量参数集合通过反复应用（循环）也能获得可比拟的表现。

Method: 结合GPT-BERT的掩码下一词预测与因果语言建模目标，引入深度参数共享机制。在748万词英文语料上训练，对比不同目标比率、非循环与循环架构、循环次数。最终模型采用4物理层进行12次循环遍历（4×12），参数量12.18M。

Result: BabyLM 2026排行榜Overall Average 35.42，NLP Average 48.48。在BLiMP和GLUE等选定语言学及下游指标上，与公开BabyLM 10M Strict-small GPT-2和GPT-BERT基线表现相当，但参数更少。消融实验显示额外循环计算能改善训练并在部分语言任务上保持强性能，但在其他任务表现较差，揭示循环设计固有局限：仅用少量物理层限制了模型的表示空间。

Conclusion: 循环架构配合参数共享可在数据受限场景下以更少参数达到与大模型相当性能，但有限的物理层数可能限制模型在某些任务上的表示能力，这是循环设计的内在权衡。

Abstract: When training data are limited, increasing parameter count is not the only way to improve language-model performance. A small parameter set, when repeatedly applied, can also deliver comparable performance. We study Looped GPT-BERT in the BabyLM 2026 Strict-small setting, combining GPT-BERT's masked next-token and causal language-modeling objectives with depth-wise parameter sharing. We train on a preprocessed 7.48M-word English corpus and compare objective ratios, non-looped and looped architectures, and loop counts. Our final $4\times12$ model uses four physical layers for twelve recurrent traversals and contains 12.18M parameters. The BabyLM 2026 leaderboard reports an Overall Average of 35.42 and an NLP Average of 48.48. Compared with public BabyLM 10M Strict-small GPT-2 and GPT-BERT baselines, it achieves comparable performance on selected linguistic and downstream metrics, including BLiMP and GLUE, with fewer parameters. The loop ablations show that additional recurrent computation can improve training and preserve strong performance on selected linguistic tasks, whereas poorer performance on other tasks may reveal an inherent limitation of the looped design: using only a few physical layers restricts the model's representational space.

</details>


### [22] [When Auditors Fabricate: Batch-Size Degradation and Confident Hallucination in LLM Detection of Planted Document Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09696)
*Karan Parekh,Sanjana Pendyala Ravinder,Sana Mhapsekar,Medina Maloku*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文评估了Google Gemini 3.0 Pro作为自动文档审计器检测植入错误的能力，发现其在小规模下表现尚可但大规模时严重失效，且失效模式为捏造而非拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越被提议用作文档质量的自动审计器，但其作为植入错误检测器的可靠性尚未得到充分表征，亟需系统性评估。

Method: 构建包含150篇学术论文的污染语料库，注入450个已知污染项（拼写腐败、语义反转、荒谬插入三类），在60篇文档上评估模型对180个污染项答案键的恢复能力，采用单文档、小批量、大批量三种提示机制。

Result: 检测率随规模崩塌：单文档50%、小批量60%、大批量仅2.8%。大规模失效表现为捏造虚假污染项（如‘心灵感应松鼠’、‘量子动力烤面包机’）。按污染类型分，荒谬插入恢复率75%，语义反转和拼写腐败各50%。最可能在真实场景出现的合理错误反而最常被漏报。

Conclusion: LLM文档审计不是优雅降级而是欺骗性失效，需建立约束机制：限制批量大小、直接注入内容、对每个报告发现进行机械式源文本验证。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly proposed as automated auditors of document quality, yet their reliability as detectors of planted errors is poorly characterised. We construct a contaminated corpus of 150 academic papers spanning supply chain management and medical research, injecting 450 known contaminants of three types: typographical corruption, semantic reversal, and absurd out-of-context insertion. We then evaluate Google Gemini 3.0 Pro's ability to recover a 180-contaminant answer-key subset across 60 documents under three prompting regimes of increasing scale: single document, small batch, and large batch. Detection holds at small scale and then collapses: 50% recovery on single documents, 60% on small batches, and 2.8% on large batches. The failure mode at scale is not abstention but fabrication. Rather than reporting incomplete processing, the model produced confident findings including invented contaminants of its own, absurdities such as "telepathic squirrel" and "quantum-powered toaster" that mimic the style of the planted material but do not appear in any document. Detection also varies by contamination type: absurd insertions were recovered at 75% in completed evaluations, while semantic reversals and typographical corruptions were each recovered at only 50%. The corruptions most likely to occur in the wild, plausible ones, are the ones most often missed. We conclude that LLM document auditing degrades not gracefully but deceptively, and outline the harness such systems require: bounded batch sizes, direct content injection, and mechanical verification of every reported finding against source text.

</details>


### [23] [Scaling E-Commerce Attribute Extraction with Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09716)
*Nikhita Vedula,Dushyanta Dhyani,Bryan Wang,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段LLM流水线，用于电商目录中的属性值提取，首先发现紧凑、排序的购买判别性属性模式，再用微调的紧凑LLM (Qwen3-4B) 配合超并行解码(HPD) 提取值，实现85%准确率且推理成本降低92%，可用于大规模生产部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商目录混乱无序，现有AVE系统将所有属性同等对待，生成庞大且不一致的属性集合，无法反映消费者区分产品的关键因素，难以支持大规模产品对比和决策。

Method: 两阶段LLM流水线：第一阶段发现每个产品类别的紧凑、排序的购买判别性属性模式；第二阶段使用微调的紧凑LLM (Qwen3-4B) 结合超并行解码(HPD) 从目录文本中提取属性值。

Result: 实现85%提取准确率，与基础LLM持平；推理成本降低92%；支持生产规模的产品发现和目录丰富；自动构建产品知识库，提供跨类别一致可比的属性。

Conclusion: 该流水线有效解决了电商属性提取的规模化与一致性问题，通过模式发现和紧凑模型部署，以极低成本实现高质量结构化产品表示，为下游知识密集型应用提供可靠基础。

Abstract: Customers rely on specific product attributes to compare products and make purchasing decisions, but e-commerce catalogs are messy and unstructured, making it difficult to identify which attributes matter most and extract them at scale. Standard Attribute Value Extraction (AVE) systems treat all attributes equally, producing large, inconsistent attribute sets that do not reflect the factors consumers use to differentiate products. We introduce a two-stage LLM pipeline that first discovers a compact, ranked schema of purchase-discriminative attributes for each product category, then extracts their values from catalog text using a fine-tuned compact LLM (Qwen3-4B) with Hyper-Parallel Decoding (HPD). This pipeline achieves 85% extraction accuracy, on par with the foundational LLM it was distilled from, while reducing inference costs by 92% over foundational LLMs, enabling production-scale use for product discovery and catalog enrichment. The resulting category-level structured representations effectively constitute automatically constructed product knowledge bases, providing consistent, comparable attributes across varied product categories that can ground downstream knowledge-intensive applications.

</details>


### [24] [StreamAlign: Streaming Text-Aligned Speech Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09719)
*Kang-wook Kim,Jinyoung Park,Jinsoo Kim,Sehun Lee,Sang Hoon Woo,Gunhee Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StreamAlign 是一种面向流式实时处理的文本对齐语音标记化框架，通过结合字符级 RNN-Transducer 对齐与词级 ASR 引导，解决了离线 ASR 导致的流式处理不可用及词汇表不匹配问题，实现了 270ms 的低延迟标记化，并在 LibriSpeech 上取得最低 WER 和最高 UTMOS，基于其训练的 StreamAlign-SLM 在语音续作与一致性基准上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本对齐语音标记化方法依赖离线 ASR，存在两大缺陷：(1) 需完整语句才能标记化，无法支持实时流式处理；(2) ASR 与 LLM 词汇表不匹配，导致声学粒度从子词降为词级，损害建模精度。因此需要一种流式标记化框架，同时保持识别准确性并解决词汇表不匹配。

Method: StreamAlign 提出在线语音-文本对齐：结合字符级 RNN-Transducer 对齐与词级 ASR 引导，缓解 ASR-LLM 词汇表不匹配并保持识别准确性；引入主动词边界分类器，在块边界预测词完成，将标记化延迟从 560ms 降至 270ms。

Result: 在 LibriSpeech 测试中，StreamAlign 实现评测标记器中最低 WER 与最高 UTMOS。基于 StreamAlign 单元训练的 StreamAlign-SLM 在语音续作任务上超越其他端到端口语语言模型，并在 SALMon 与口语 StoryCloze 基准上取得最强整体一致性。

Conclusion: StreamAlign 成功实现了低延迟的流式文本对齐语音标记化，有效解决了离线 ASR 的流式限制与词汇表不匹配问题，显著提升了实时语音-文本联合建模的性能，为端到端口语语言模型提供了更优的标记化基础。

Abstract: Text-aligned speech tokenization methods have emerged to better align speech tokens with LLM token spaces, enabling more effective utilization of pretrained LLMs. However, they rely on offline automatic speech recognition (ASR), leading to two key limitations: (i) the need for complete utterances before tokenization, precluding real-time streaming, and (ii) vocabulary mismatch between ASR and LLMs, which reduces acoustic granularity from the subword to the word level. We introduce StreamAlign, a text-aligned speech tokenization framework that enables streaming tokenization for real-time speech-text joint modeling. StreamAlign performs online speech-text alignment by combining character-level RNN-Transducer alignment with word-level ASR guidance, mitigating ASR-LLM vocabulary mismatch while preserving recognition accuracy. A proactive word boundary classifier anticipates word completion at chunk boundaries, reducing tokenization latency from 560 ms to 270 ms. On LibriSpeech, StreamAlign achieves the lowest WER and highest UTMOS among evaluated tokenizers. Furthermore, StreamAlign-SLM, a spoken language model trained on StreamAlign units, outperforms other end-to-end spoken language models in speech continuation while achieving the strongest overall consistency on SALMon and spoken StoryCloze.

</details>


### [25] [SocialRL: Refining LLMs' Social Intelligence through Multi-turn Reinforcement Learning and Reward Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09764)
*Jianing Wang,Xintao Wang,Aili Chen,Jie Shi,Hongcheng Guo,Jun Gao,Wenxuan Zhao,Chengkun Lang,Yuanli Guo,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SocialRL，一种多轮强化学习框架，通过 PPO 传播延迟奖励并设计六个过程奖励维度（目标推进、关系共鸣、上下文连贯等）配合阶段感知权重调度，显著提升了语言模型在社交对话中的目标达成率（平均 +9.2%），验证了其在合成与真实、标准与挑战性社交场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法仅优化单轮话语和稀疏结果奖励，导致短视策略难以处理多轮交互中的目标与关系张力；随着语言模型成为自主协作者，社交智能（读取社交语境、推断意图、长程自适应）成为构建高效可信人机交互的核心。

Method: SocialRL 包含两大创新：1) 采用多轮 PPO 将延迟的结果奖励回传到每一轮，实现长时域规划；2) 设计六大过程奖励维度（目标推进、关系共鸣、上下文连贯等）捕捉目标-关系权衡，奖励模型动态生成细粒度评分准则，并引入阶段感知权重调度——早期侧重关系建立、中期侧重目标推进、后期平衡收尾。

Result: 在多个社交对话基准上，SocialRL 相对基础模型将目标达成率平均提升 9.2 个百分点，且在合成与真实、标准与挑战性社交场景中均表现优异。

Conclusion: SocialRL 通过多轮奖励回传与动态过程奖励建模，有效解决了社交对话中的长时域规划与目标-关系权衡问题，证明了其在多样化社交场景下的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Social intelligence enables agents to read social context, infer intent, and adapt over sustained dialogue. As language models become autonomous collaborators, it is central to building effective and trustworthy human-AI interaction. Existing reinforcement learning methods optimize single-turn utterances and sparse outcome rewards, producing short-sighted policies that struggle to manage goal-relationship tensions across multi-turn interactions. We propose SocialRL, a multi-turn reinforcement learning framework addressing both challenges. First, we apply multi-turn reinforcement learning using PPO that propagates delayed outcome rewards back to each turn, enabling long-horizon planning. Second, we design six process reward dimensions capturing the goal-relationship trade-off, including goal advancement, relational attunement, contextual coherence, etc. A reward model dynamically generates fine-grained scoring criteria for each dimension, while a stage-aware weight schedule prioritizes relationship-building in early turns, goal advancement mid-way, and balanced closure late. Across multiple social-dialogue benchmarks, SocialRL improves Goal Achievement by an average of 9.2 percentage points over the corresponding Base models. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of SocialRL across synthetic and real social scenes, as well as standard and challenging social scenarios.

</details>


### [26] [CARRE: Counterfactual Action Retrieval and Reason Evaluation for Explainable Churn Prescription](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09766)
*Minjoo Kim,Sangjin Park,Seung Hwan Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CARRE是一个三阶段流失处方框架，结合检索增强候选生成、成本感知反事实评分和大语言模型推理，实现保留动作的选择与解释，在IBM Telco数据集上显著优于SHAP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流失模型仅识别高风险客户，未指明具体可行的保留动作及其适用性理由，需要一种能够生成可执行保留建议并提供结构化解释的方法。

Method: CARRE包含三阶段：1) 检索增强候选生成，从预定义保留动作目录中检索候选；2) 成本感知反事实评分，通过显式特征变换估计模型预测的流失风险变化；3) 大语言模型推理，生成结构化流失原因和基于用户画像的动作解释。

Result: 在313个高风险测试案例上，CARRE比普通SHAP基线平均模型预测风险降低提高79.8%，比成本控制SHAP+Cost基线提高80.4%，成本归一化效率高10.5%；在136个原因分层样本上，诊断驱动提示优化使弱标签一致性从79.4%提升至90.4%，无约束违规；135个解释获两LLM裁判均分4.02-5.00/5，确定性审计发现66个可验证画像声明无矛盾；检索消融显示k=5在候选覆盖率与下游推理一致性间取得最佳折衷。

Conclusion: 研究展示了检索、基于模型的反事实评分和语言生成可在原型流失处方管道中分离并联合评估，为可解释、可执行的客户保留决策提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Churn models typically identify high-risk customers but do not specify which feasible retention action should be considered or why that action is appropriate. We present CARRE (Counterfactual Action Retrieval and Reason Evaluation), a three-stage framework that combines retrieval-augmented candidate generation, cost-aware counterfactual scoring, and large language model (LLM) reasoning. CARRE retrieves a predefined catalog of retention actions, estimates model-predicted churn-risk changes under explicit feature transformations, and generates a structured churn reason and a profile-grounded explanation for the selected action. On the IBM Telco Customer Churn dataset, CARRE achieves 79.8% greater mean model-predicted risk reduction than the plain SHAP baseline and 80.4% greater reduction than the cost-controlled SHAP+Cost baseline across 313 high-risk test cases; its cost-normalized efficiency is 10.5% higher than that of plain SHAP. On a 136-case reason-stratified evaluation sample, diagnosis-driven prompt refinement increases weak-label agreement from 79.4% to 90.4%, with no auxiliary-plan constraint violations; because the same sample was used for error diagnosis and re-evaluation, the post-refinement result is not an independent estimate of generalization. For 135 explanations generated using the pre-refinement v2 reason outputs, two cross-vendor LLM judges assign mean scores ranging from 4.02 to 5.00 out of 5, although one judge saturates on actionability, and a deterministic audit finds no contradictions among 66 verifiable profile claims. Retrieval ablations show that k=5 provides the best evaluated compromise between high candidate coverage and downstream reasoning agreement in this dataset. These results illustrate how retrieval, model-based counterfactual scoring, and language generation can be separated and jointly evaluated in a prototype churn-prescription pipeline.

</details>


### [27] [SymbolicLight V2: Hybrid Neuromorphic Architecture and Sparse Execution for Low-Energy Language Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09772)
*Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SymbolicLight V2 是一种混合神经形态语言架构，结合稀疏事件计算与连续状态处理，在 FPGA 和 ARM CPU 上实现 1.94 亿参数模型，通过主动行权重收集和有效状态 KV 加载显著提升解码吞吐并降低能耗，但模型质量落后于同预算稠密基线，未能证明同质量下的能效优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过神经形态稀疏事件驱动计算减少冗余计算和数据移动，从而在专用硬件（FPGA、ARM CPU）上实现大语言模型的高能效推理，解决传统稠密矩阵乘法在边缘设备上的功耗和延迟问题。

Method: 扩展 V1 的脉冲门控双路径，引入分级符号事件和无 softmax 局部注意力；在 Alveo U50C FPGA 上采用定点运算，在 ARM Cortex-A76 上采用稀疏整数执行；采用主动行权重收集和有效状态 KV 加载优化解码流程；固定检查点进行同配置对比实验。

Result: FPGA 上解码吞吐从 474.6 提升至 643.2 tokens/s（32-token 前缀，128 输出），单 token 能耗降低 27.6%（0.06087→0.04407 J），完整请求能耗降低 24.4-27.7%；空闲功耗占总能耗 82.8%，验证低延迟带来的能效收益；相比 RTX 5090 FP32 基线，FPGA 整数执行解码能耗降低 89.1%；ARM 四核达 65.4 tokens/s，功耗 9.80 W，单 token 能耗 0.151 J（AC 输入）。

Conclusion: 事件稀疏性可直接转化为计算和数据移动的省略，吞吐提升幅度大于有源功率增幅时可降低单 token 能耗；但由于部署检查点质量不及同预算稠密模型，实验结果未能确立同等模型质量下的能效优势，后续需在保持质量前提下验证架构效能。

Abstract: SymbolicLight V2 combines sparse event computation with continuous-state processing in a hybrid neuromorphic language architecture. Extending V1's spike-gated dual paths, it adds graded signed events at further projections and softmax-free local attention. We implement the 194M-parameter model on an Alveo U50C FPGA using digital fixed-point arithmetic and on an ARM CPU using sparse integer execution. Across three same-checkpoint FPGA implementations at 175 MHz, active-row weight gathering and valid-state KV loading raise decode throughput from 474.6 to 643.2 tokens/s for a 32-token prefix and 128 outputs. Estimated gross card energy falls from 0.06087 to 0.04407 J per generated token, a 27.6% reduction. Complete-request energy, including prefill, falls by 24.4-27.7% across three prefix lengths. An independent idle split attributes 82.8% of gross card energy to loaded idle, explaining the benefit of shorter token latency. Against the recorded RTX 5090 compiled-FP32 baseline, integer FPGA execution uses 89.1% less estimated card energy during short-context decode; arithmetic precisions differ, and the GPU baseline is not the lowest-energy tested configuration. On four Cortex-A76 cores of a ROCK 5T, complete requests reach 65.4 tokens/s at 9.80 W and 0.151 J per generated token at the adapter's AC input. These results connect event sparsity to omitted computation and data movement. The mechanisms also support other dedicated V2 implementations: increasing throughput by a greater factor than active power lowers energy per generated token. Evaluation holds the deployed checkpoint fixed; its quality trails a same-budget dense control, so the results do not establish equal-quality efficiency.

</details>


### [28] [ROAM: Robust Organization of Atomic Memories for Agents through Semantic Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09778)
*Jianjie Zheng,Peng Lai,Sijie Cheng,Jiehui Zhao,Lei Yang,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ROAM 是一个基于关系的原子记忆管理框架，通过分类原子间关系（独立、等价、方向性包含、冲突）并组织为主要视图和证据视图，融合互补细节以提升长期语言模型代理的回答准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期语言模型代理依赖外部记忆，原子记忆因其细粒度语义边界便于精确检索和比较，但积累的原子会变得冗余、重叠或冲突。现有方法让 LLM 管理器直接增删改记忆，将语义理解、存储决策、内容生成耦合在一个易出错的操作中。

Method: ROAM 将新旧原子对分类为四种关系（独立、等价、方向性包含、冲突），将观察组织为活跃的 Primary（主要）和支持性的 Evidence（证据）角色。融合步骤将互补细节和时间变化合并为紧凑的非原子视图。仅检索 Primary 视图用于回答，避免冗余或过时原子独立竞争。

Result: 在多种模型和评估设置下，ROAM 将回答准确率提升高达 29.8 个百分点。消融实验显示不同关系互补受益，融合在角色组织之外持续带来增益。机制分析发现答案关键来源召回率提高 15.6 点，干扰 token 占比降低 11.5 点。ROAM 在不同管理器规模下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: ROAM 通过关系引导的原子记忆管理和融合机制，有效解决了原子记忆积累的冗余、冲突问题，显著提升长期代理的回答准确性和检索质量，且具有良好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Long-term language-model agents rely on external memory across interactions. Atomic memories are particularly useful: their fine-grained semantic boundaries enable precise retrieval and direct comparison between observations. Yet accumulating atoms inevitably become redundant, overlapping, or conflicting. Existing methods often ask an LLM manager to add, update, delete, or rewrite memories directly, coupling semantic interpretation, storage decisions, and content generation in one error-prone operation. We introduce ROAM, a relation-guided framework that uses atomicity for management while allowing richer answer-time representations. ROAM classifies incoming--stored atom pairs as independent, equivalent, directionally subsuming, or conflicting, then organizes observations into active Primary and supporting Evidence roles. Fusion subsequently combines complementary details and temporal changes into compact, potentially non-atomic views. Only Primary views are retrieved for answering, preventing redundant or outdated atoms from competing independently. Across models and evaluation settings, ROAM improves answer accuracy by up to 29.8 percentage points. Ablations show complementary benefits from different relations and consistent gains from fusion beyond role organization. Mechanism analysis further finds 15.6-point higher answer-critical source recall and an 11.5-point lower confounder-token share. ROAM remains robust across manager scales.

</details>


### [29] [MUCnoHARM@GermEval Shared Task 2026: Retrieval-based In-Context Learning for Defamatory Offences, and Where It Falls Short](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09791)
*Kristin Gnadt,Maximilian Meidinger,Matthias Aßenmacher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于检索的上下文学习策略在检测德国刑法典第185-187条诽谤犯罪方面的效果。主要发现：小样本提示优于零样本，但基于检索的方法仅比随机演示略好，甚至不如优化的静态演示集；提供具体法律知识有帮助，但模型选择比其他所有系统选择都更重要；模型过度预测犯罪相关性，同时仍漏检26-57%的犯罪相关帖子，适合用于分诊而非自动化审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络仇恨言论普遍存在，自动检测至关重要，特别是针对具有刑事相关性的社交媒体帖子。

Method: 研究多种基于检索的上下文学习策略，用于检测德国刑法典第185-187条规定的诽谤犯罪（GermEval 2026子任务4）。

Result: 小样本提示优于零样本；基于检索的方法仅比随机演示有边际提升，甚至落后于优化的静态演示集；提供具体法律知识有帮助；模型选择比其他所有系统选择都更重要；模型过度预测犯罪相关性，同时仍漏检26-57%的犯罪相关帖子。

Conclusion: 当前模型适合用于分诊筛选，而非完全自动化的内容审核。

Abstract: With hate speech being ubiquitous online, automatic detection is crucial, in particular when it comes to criminally relevant social media posts. We study a variety of retrieval-based in-context learning (RetICL) strategies for detecting defamatory offences under §§ 185-187 StGB (the subject of GermEval 2026 Subtask 4). Few-shot prompting beats zero-shot, but retrieval-based approaches offer only marginal gains over random demonstrations, and even fall behind an optimised static set of demonstrations. Providing concrete legal knowledge helps, yet model choice outweighs every other system choice. Models over-predict criminal relevance while still missing 26-57% of criminally relevant posts, suiting them for triage rather than autonomous moderation.

</details>


### [30] [HyperTrace: Hypothesis-Based Preference Tracing for Online LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09835)
*Jianzhi Shen,Keyu Mao,Minghao Shao,Chuanyang Jin,Yusong Wang,Ailiang Lin,Kotaro Funakoshi,Manabu Okumura,Tianmin Shu,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyperTrace是一个无需训练的在线个性化框架，通过维护短期意图和长期偏好的自然语言假设，并采用SMC风格的重加权过程更新这些假设，实现无参数更新的用户偏好追踪，在PRISM和PersonaMem-v2上显著提升响应对齐、偏好预测和档案一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练个性化方法依赖存储历史或检索记忆，难以协调长期偏好与短期话题需求，且用户偏好隐含且逐步显现，需要一种能在线追踪潜在偏好的方法。

Method: 提出HyperTrace框架，将在线个性化建模为潜在偏好追踪：维护可解释的自然语言假设（短期意图和长期偏好），利用基于LLM的代理选择模型，通过SMC（顺序蒙特卡洛）风格的重加权过程跨轮次和会话更新假设，实现无参数更新的个性化。

Result: 在PRISM和PersonaMem-v2基准上，HyperTrace优于强在线基线，提升了响应对齐度、偏好预测准确性和档案一致性。

Conclusion: 追踪用户潜在偏好是实现鲁棒个性化的有效途径，HyperTrace通过假设维护与SMC更新机制，在无训练条件下实现了长短期偏好的协调与持续适应。

Abstract: Personalized language models aim to adapt responses to individual users, whose preferences are often latent and revealed gradually through interaction. Existing training-free methods rely on stored histories or retrieved memories, but they often struggle to reconcile long- term preferences with short-term topic-specific needs. To address this issue, we propose HyperTrace, a training-free framework that formulates online personalization as latent preference tracing. HyperTrace maintains interpretable natural-language hypotheses over short-term intent and long-term preferences, and updates them through an SMC-style reweight process using an LLM-based surrogate choice model. By updating these hypotheses across turns and sessions, HyperTrace enables personalization without parameter updates. Experiments on PRISM and PersonaMem-v2 show that HyperTrace improves response alignment, preference prediction, and profile consistency over strong online baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of tracing latent user preferences for robust personalization. Code and scripts are available in the repository: https://github.com/jiseshen/HyperTrace.

</details>


### [31] [$S^3$-Bench: Evaluating Speech Interaction Models as Scientific Voice Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09852)
*Heyang Liu,Jiayi Huang,Wenyang Xiao,Ziyang Cheng,Lixin Zhang,Zhen Liu,Miao He,Ronghua Wu,Qunshan Gu,Yanfeng Wang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了S³-Bench，首个针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在科学领域语音交互能力的系统性评测框架，覆盖10大学科，包含知识问答集和多轮对话集，通过将原子轮次分解为语音识别、感知、知识推理、发音四个阶段，系统揭示了现有模型在专业术语、缩写规范、符号表达等方面的挑战与性能权衡，并发现多轮交互中存在用户适应、响应准确性、完整性和效率等持久性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型在通用语音助手任务上表现优异，但在科学等专业领域的语音交互能力尚未被充分探索。科学交互面临罕见技术术语、口语缩写规范、符号特殊表达的自然口语化等严峻挑战，缺乏系统性的评测基准来量化和分析模型在这些场景下的表现。

Method: 构建S³-Bench评测框架，涵盖10大科学学科，包含两个子集：(1) Knowledge set：用于语音问答评测；(2) Dialogue set：用于模拟用户代理的多轮渐进式交互评测。将完整的原子交互轮次分解为四个阶段：语音识别(ASR)、感知、知识利用与推理、响应发音(TTS)，从而系统表征各阶段的挑战与性能权衡。

Result: 实验系统表征了现有MLLMs在科学语音交互中的常见挑战和性能权衡。多轮交互实验揭示了模型在用户适应能力、生成准确/完整/高效响应方面存在持久性局限。通过阶段分解分析，量化了语音识别、感知、推理、发音各环节的瓶颈。

Conclusion: S³-Bench为科学领域MLLMs语音交互能力提供了首个系统性评测基准，揭示了当前模型在专业术语处理、符号口语化、多轮用户适应等方面的根本性不足，为未来针对性改进提供了细粒度的诊断依据和评测标准。

Abstract: The advance of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has fundamentally reshaped the paradigm of human-computer interaction, especially speech interaction models capable of seamless conversations. Despite remarkable performance as general voice assistants, their performance in specialized domains remains underexplored, particularly in scientific areas. Scientific interactions introduce formidable challenges, involving rare technical terminology, spoken norms of abbreviations, and the natural verbalization of symbolic special expressions. In this paper, we introduce S$^3$-Bench, a systematic evaluation framework covering 10 major disciplines, consisting of a Knowledge set for speech question-answering and a Dialogue set for multi-turn progressive interactions with simulated user agents. By decomposing a complete atomic turn into stages of speech recognition, perception, knowledge utilization with reasoning, and response pronunciation, we systematically characterize the common challenges and performance tradeoffs of existing approaches. Furthermore, experiments on multi-turn interactions reveal persistent limitations in user adaptation and the generation of accurate, comprehensive, and efficient responses.

</details>


### [32] [When Does Defendant Statement Matter? A Study of Bias and Persuasion in LLM-Simulated Jurors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09887)
*Cho-Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 JuryBench 基准，研究 LLM 模拟陪审员在普通法陪审团审判中的决策行为，重点考察被告庭审陈述的说服力、意识形态偏见和背景亲和力对判决严厉度的影响，实验涉及 20 个前沿 LLMs，共 432K 判决，发现情感说服可能适得其反，背景匹配比其他孤立因素影响更大，陪审员对异背景被告更严厉、同背景更宽容，意识形态强烈塑造严厉度判断，揭示 LLM 模拟陪审推理的潜力与风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽用 LLMs 模拟专业场景下的人类决策，但普通法陪审团审判中的 LLM 行为尚未探索；需要系统评估被告庭审陈述（情感诉求、反驳）、被告背景、陪审员意识形态等因素如何影响 LLM 模拟陪审员的裁决，以验证 LLM 是否能复现人类陪审团研究发现，并揭示其偏见与局限。

Method: 构建 JuryBench 基准，包含美国刑法中有争议的刑事案件，固定基础案情，设计不同背景的被告，其庭审陈述包含不同程度的情感诉求或反驳；模拟具有跨光谱意识形态概况的陪审员；测试 20 个前沿 LLMs，共生成 432K 个判决及理由；量化判决严厉度的变化，分析说服、意识形态偏见、背景亲和力的影响。

Result: 1) 情感说服可能有害，陪审员可能将其视为有罪或前后不一致的证据。2) 陪审员与被告的背景匹配是比其他孤立因素更强且显著的因素，陪审员总体上对异背景被告更严厉、对同背景被告更宽容。3) 陪审员意识形态也强烈塑造严厉度判断。LLM 陪审团模拟在许多方面呼应了人类陪审团研究发现。

Conclusion: 研究表明 LLM 模拟陪审推理既有潜力也有风险，能复现人类陪审团的关键行为模式（如情感说服的反效果、背景亲和力、意识形态影响），但也暴露了偏见放大等风险；呼吁在实际部署前进行谨慎评估。数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: LLMs have been used to simulate human decision-making in professional settings, yet their behaviors in common-law jury trials remain unexplored. We study when and how a defendant's courtroom statement affects LLM-simulated jurors, focusing on persuasion, ideological bias, and background-based affinity. To support the analysis, we introduce JuryBench, a benchmark containing controversial criminal cases in U.S. criminal law. In each case, a defendant can claim various plausible justifications to support acquittal or reduced liability. We fix the base case and design defendants of different backgrounds, who give courtroom statements with varying emotional appeal or rebuttal. Jurors with diverse ideological profiles across the spectrum are simulated. We examine 20 frontier LLMs, resulting in a total of 432K decisions and rationales, and quantify changes in verdict severity. Our findings show that LLM-jury simulation echoes many human-jury findings. First, emotional persuasion can be detrimental, since jurors may perceive it as evidence of guilt or inconsistency. Next, we show that background fit between jurors and defendants is a stronger and significant factor than other isolated factors, and that jurors are in general harsher toward opposite-background defendants and lenient toward same-background ones. Finally, we find that juror ideology also strongly shapes severity judgments. These findings highlight both the promise and risks of using LLMs to model jury reasoning and call for careful evaluation. The data and code are available at https://github.com/choyingw/JuryBench

</details>


### [33] [Leveraging Fine-grained Error Correction in Korean Speech Recognition for Consultation Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09889)
*Yonghyun Jun,Jimin Lee,Hwan Chang,Dongho Shin,Seolah Kim,Hwanhee Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建首个大规模韩语对话级ASR纠错基准数据集DasanCallDial（1974段对话、115460轮话语），并提出Detector-Gated Contextual Span Correction (DCSC)框架：先用编码器做token级错误检测，再用语言模型做span级精细纠错，融合对话上下文增强，在低资源纯文本后编辑场景下达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实呼叫中心场景下ASR模型必然存在错误，隐私限制导致无法访问音频，必须依赖纯文本后编辑。现有纯文本方法在低资源语言（尤其是韩语）面临标注语料极度稀缺、缺乏针对性纠错方法的问题，现有韩语资源多面向ASR训练而非文本纠错，亟需专用基准与方法。

Method: 1. 数据构建：从真实呼叫中心交互中提炼DasanCallDial数据集。2. DCSC框架：(a) 编码器检测器进行token级错误检测；(b) 语言模型纠错器针对检测到的span级错误进行精细纠错；(c) 对话级上下文增强，利用话语历史消歧。采用多粒度（token检测+span纠错）联合建模。

Result: 在DasanCallDial基准上，DCSC实现最优性能，显著超越通用大语言模型在低资源设定下的表现，验证了多粒度建模与对话上下文融合的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过填补韩语ASR文本纠错的资源空白，提出首个对话级基准与DCSC框架，证明在纯文本、低资源约束下，结合token级检测、span级纠错与对话上下文可有效提升纠错质量，为低资源语言ASR后编辑提供可复用范式。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) technology is fundamental to customer service automation and large-scale transcription. However, even advanced ASR models exhibit inevitable errors in complex real-world environments such as call center conversations. When privacy restrictions preclude audio access, error correction must rely on text-based post-editing. Existing text-only approaches face significant challenges in low-resource languages, mainly due to a critical scarcity of annotated corpora and tailored correction methodologies. For Korean, this resource gap is particularly pronounced, as existing resources are predominantly designed for ASR training rather than text-based error correction. To address this, we introduce DasanCallDial, the first large-scale Korean benchmark dataset specifically curated for dialogue-level ASR error correction. Derived from genuine call center interactions, it comprises 1,974 dialogues with 115,460 utterances. Leveraging this resource, we propose Detector-Gated Contextual Span Correction (DCSC), a text-only post-editing framework for error-sparse Korean speech recognition transcripts. DCSC combines an encoder-based detector that first performs token-level error detection, followed by a language model-based corrector trained to rectify fine-grained span-level errors. Additionally, we employ dialogue-level context augmentation to enable the model to leverage discourse history for disambiguation. By employing multi-level granularity, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, effectively overcoming the limitations of general LLMs in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [34] [Deep and shallow biases in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09901)
*An Vo,Vy Tuong Dang,Khai-Nguyen Nguyen,Emilio Villa-Cueva,Thamar Solorio,Anh Totti Nguyen,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了'偏见深度'概念，区分大语言模型中稳定的预训练偏见（深层偏见）和提示词依赖的偏见（浅层偏见），发现仅约1/4的集中偏好在重述场景下存活，且深层偏见更难通过微调和提示词去偏移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将模型倾向同一答案视为偏见，但未区分稳定的模型偏好与提示词措辞导致的响应差异，需要一种度量同时捕捉直接提示下的偏好强度和跨场景重述的稳定性。

Method: 引入偏见深度分数，测量模型在直接提示下对首选答案的偏好强度及其在场景重述下的存活率；在4,442个观点提示和四个大语言模型上实验，对比持续微调与基于提示词的去偏方法对深层/浅层偏见的移除难度。

Result: 仅约四分之一的集中偏好在重述中存活（深层偏见），其余为提示词依赖的浅层偏见；深层偏见更多源自预训练并经SFT保留，在持续微调和提示词去偏下均更难移除。

Conclusion: 偏见深度有效分离了稳定的习得偏见与提示词伪影，单一提示指标会混淆二者；深层偏见具有更强的鲁棒性和遗传性，需针对性设计去偏策略。

Abstract: Large language models often repeatedly select the same answer even when many alternatives are plausible. Prior work treats this concentration as bias, but it does not distinguish stable model preferences from responses that depend on a particular prompt wording. We introduce a bias depth score that measures both how strongly a model prefers its top answer under direct prompting and whether that answer survives scenario reframing. Across 4,442 opinion prompts and four large language models, only about a quarter of the concentrated preferences survive reframing. We call these persistent cases Deep biases, and the remaining prompt-dependent cases Shallow biases. Our results show that Deep biases are more often inherited from pretraining and preserved through SFT. Under both continued fine-tuning and prompt-based debiasing for diversity, Deep biases are consistently harder to remove than Shallow biases. Bias depth therefore separates stable learned biases from prompt-wording artifacts that single-prompt metrics conflate. Code, models, and data are available at deepbias.github.io.

</details>


### [35] [Contrastive Projection: Reading Transformer Internals by Differencing Logit Lenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09902)
*Olli Tuomi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种通过差异读取Transformer内部状态的新方法：对比两个紧密匹配的提示词的隐藏状态差异并投影到词表空间，从而抵消共享成分、凸显区别性特征。该方法构建了一个无需训练的追踪器，在Phi-2中发现了复合名词的MLP->注意力链路，并在三种架构中通过读出和探针验证了相同区别的可恢复性。跨种子控制实验显示，相同的计算区别在不同初始化的网络中表现为几乎完全不同的token（top-10重叠仅0.08），揭示了token空间表征的网络特异性，而其编码的区别则是通用的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的logit lens方法直接读取单层隐藏状态在token空间的投影，但在中间层主要反映模型对几乎任何输入都会预测的通用token，难以可靠地揭示模型内部的具体计算区别。需要一种能抵消共享背景、凸显差异性计算的读取方法。
