Conclusion: OntologyAligner 提供了一个通用、高精度的生物医学文本到本体概念映射框架，通过检索-重排-层级细化三阶段协同有效解决了词汇变异与层级细粒度区分难题，并发布了大规模统一基准 PhenoNormBench，推动领域可复现研究与应用落地。

Abstract: Biomedical ontology normalization maps free-text expressions to standardized concepts, enabling consistent integration and analysis of biomedical data. This task remains challenging because lexical variation and subtle distinctions among hierarchically related concepts can obscure concept boundaries. We present OntologyAligner, a three-stage framework that combines ontology-aligned retrieval, large language model candidate reranking, and selective hierarchy-guided refinement. We also construct PhenoNormBench, a unified benchmark comprising 13,390 samples from seven Human Phenotype Ontology datasets. OntologyAligner achieved state-of-the-art performance on HPO normalization, with 88.78% Macro Top-1 Accuracy and 86.75% Micro Top-1 Accuracy, exceeding the strongest baseline by 4.85 and 5.07 percentage points, respectively. Ablation analyses showed complementary contributions from all three stages, and sensitivity analyses demonstrated stability across candidate-set sizes and model backbones. Applications to MONDO, MEDIC, and NCBITaxon further established portability to other ontologies. OntologyAligner offers a generalizable framework for accurate mapping of biomedical text to structured ontology concepts. PhenoNormBench and the code are publicly available at https://github.com/zhelishisongjie/OntologyAligner.

</details>


### [106] [Reference-Based Bias Detection in LLMs via Relative Representations of Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10060)
*Marek Jeliński,Jan Dubiński,Maciej Chrabaszcz,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于参考句的隐藏状态表示偏见审计方法（ΔB），通过将句子编码为与固定锚点句子的相似度，构建共享比较空间，量化目标群体与正负属性关联的偏移。实验显示ΔB与输出层面偏见变化高度相关（|r|=0.84），能以极低算力（3-50倍更少）在约3分钟内完成审计，且优于基于SEAT的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见审计方法依赖模型输出，需要昂贵的基准或裁判模型，且可能遗漏未显性化于生成文本中的内部表示偏移。作者旨在开发一种无需任务特定评估数据、计算高效、且能捕捉模型内部表示几何变化的偏见审计方法。

Method: 提出基于参考的相对表示方法：选取固定锚点句子集合，将每个句子编码为其与锚点句子的相似度向量，从而在共享比较空间中获得相对表示。定义表示偏见偏移量ΔB，测量目标群体在该空间中与正向/负向属性关联的相对位移。对比全量微调和参数高效微调下的ΔB与输出层面偏见变化的相关性。

Result: 在三个模型家族和三个基准上，ΔB在18个设置中有15个与输出层面偏见变化显著相关（全量微调下|r|=0.84，p<0.001）。阈值化ΔB可检测偏见增加的检查点，ROC AUC在0.65-0.99之间，在WildGuardMix和DecodingTrust上优于SEAT基线。ΔB对锚点集、属性集和目标模板变化稳健。审计耗时约3分钟，算力仅为输出层面基准的3-50分之一。

Conclusion: ΔB是一种高效、无需标注数据的内部表示偏见审计方法，能可靠指示输出层面偏见变化，适合作为输出层面审计的补充而非替代。其稳健性和低计算成本使其适用于大规模模型变体的快速偏筛。

Abstract: Existing bias auditing methods typically rely on model outputs, requiring costly benchmarks or judge models and potentially missing internal shifts that never appear in generated text. We propose a reference-based method that audits bias in hidden-state representations across related model variants, for example before and after fine-tuning. Because fine-tuning reshapes representation geometry, absolute hidden states are not directly comparable, so we encode each sentence by its similarities to a fixed set of anchor sentences, yielding relative representations in a shared comparison space. There we measure how target groups shift in their association with positive and negative attributes, a quantity we call the Representational Bias Shift $ΔB$. Across three model families and the WildGuardMix, DecodingTrust and ToxiGen benchmarks, $ΔB$ correlates with output-level bias change in 15 of the 18 settings we test, reaching $|r| = 0.84$ ($p < 0.001$) under full fine-tuning and becoming more model-dependent under parameter-efficient adaptation. Thresholding $ΔB$ detects checkpoints whose bias increased with ROC AUC between $0.65$ and $0.99$, and on WildGuardMix and DecodingTrust it separates them better than a SEAT-based baseline for all three families. $ΔB$ is also stable under changes to the anchor set, attribute sets and target templates. Our method requires no task-specific evaluation data and audits a model in about three minutes, using $3$-$50\times$ less compute than the output-level benchmarks considered here. We view it as complementary to output-based auditing rather than a replacement for it.

</details>


### [107] [RAP: Research Attention Prediction Reveals Target-Conditioned Evidence Acquisition Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10092)
*Yingqian Wu,Jingcong Liang,Siyuan Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Philip Torr,Junchi Yu,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Research Attention Prediction (RAP)基准评估LLM作为研究代理跟踪研究关注度变化的能力，发现搜索虽有帮助但诊断模型仍不及EWMA基线，识别出状态前向传递优于直接预测、预测导向策略检索近期证据较少等瓶颈，微调可提升预测相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LLM作为研究代理跟踪研究关注度转移的能力，现有评估缺乏可唯一验证的结果，需建立滚动基准量化预测未来六个月论文份额分布的能力。

Method: 构建RAP基准覆盖278个AI/ML领域和1390个回合，LLM代理在时间受限arXiv语料库中搜索并预测八个冻结研究方向未来六个月论文份额；对比EWMA基线，分析状态前向传递vs直接预测、冻结证据重放、精确历史活动下的未来特定更新能力；微调Qwen3-4B于实现结果。

Result: 搜索有帮助但四个诊断模型组合准确率均不及EWMA基线；累积历史访问下状态前向传递优于直接预测；冻结证据重放揭示预测导向策略检索近期证据份额较小；即使有精确历史活动，未来特定更新仍受限，仅GPT-5.5加重开搜索略超EWMA；微调使Qwen3-4B在后期源的保留领域预测Spearman相关性提升0.105，变化丰富回合也有增益。

Conclusion: RAP基准揭示LLM作为研究代理在跟踪研究关注度动态方面存在系统性瓶颈，需改进证据检索策略和时间推理能力；微调历史结果可部分缓解预测局限，但未来特定更新仍是核心挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as research agents, yet their ability to track shifts in research attention is difficult to evaluate because reviews and research ideas lack uniquely verifiable outcomes. We introduce Research Attention Prediction (RAP), a rolling benchmark covering 278 AI/ML fields and 1,390 episodes. At each cut-off, an LLM agent searches a temporally restricted arXiv corpus and predicts the next six months' paper shares across eight frozen research directions. Search generally helps, but all four diagnostic models perform worse than an exact-count exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA) baseline in compositional accuracy. We identify two linked bottlenecks. Under cumulative-history access, State carry-forward outperforms direct Forecast for all four diagnostic models; frozen-evidence replay links a shared component of this reversal to Forecast-oriented policies retrieving a smaller share of recent evidence. Even with exact historical activity, future-specific updating remains limited, with only GPT-5.5 plus reopened Search slightly surpassing EWMA. Fine-tuning on realised outcomes improves Qwen3-4B's forecast Spearman correlation by 0.105 on held-out fields at later origins, with gains also on change-rich episodes.

</details>


### [108] [Agent-Based ML-LLM Fusion with Self-Optimizing Prompts for Plateau Weather Alerts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10135)
*Shuai Yan,Yang Xu,Shan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SmartWeatherAgent三阶段架构，融合规则与大语言模型实现多粒度查询解析，采用融入高原特征的LightGBM进行灾害预测，并通过12轮微步提示自我优化显著提升预警质量，实现结构化、可解释、科学严谨的气象预警自动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对旅游气象服务中存在的情境化不足、泛化能力弱、场景适应性差等问题，旨在构建主动感知、可解释决策、智能代理的新一代气象服务体系。

Method: 设计统一的三阶段架构：意图识别、灾害预测、推理增强生成。融合规则方法与大语言模型解析多粒度查询；引入高原特有特征（如风速突变率）的LightGBM模型预测强风、降水、低温事件；建立12轮微步提示自我优化循环，迭代优化预警生成质量。

Result: LightGBM模型在三类灾害上达到F1-Macro 0.605，推理延迟仅1.60 ms。经12轮优化，预警综合质量分S_final从4.2（B01）跃升至8.9（B12，+112%）。关键提升：B08数据源引用分6.5→8.5，B10物理机制解释持续高分，B12科学严谨性峰值达9.2（显式不确定性陈述）。

Conclusion: 系统能自主生成融合因果机制、时空演变、定量证据、法规引用、置信度陈述的结构化预警，显著增强专业深度、逻辑严谨性与科学可信度，推动气象服务向主动感知、可解释决策、智能代理方向演进。

Abstract: To address insufficient contextualization, weak generalization, and poor scenario adaptation in tourism meteorological services, we propose SmartWeatherAgent--a unified three-stage architecture integrating intent recognition, hazard prediction, and reasoning-enhanced generation. The system fuses rule-based methods with large language models to parse queries at multiple granularities and employs a LightGBM model enriched with highland-specific features (e.g., wind speed abruptness rate), achieving an F1-Macro score of 0.605 with 1.60 ms latency on high-wind, precipitation, and low-temperature events. A 12-round micro-step prompt self-optimization loop boosts the composite warning quality score S_final from 4.2 (B01) to 8.9 (B12, +112%). Key improvements include a sharp rise in B08 from data source citation (6.5 -> 8.5), sustained high performance in B10 via physical mechanism explanation, and a peak scientific rigor score of 9.2 in B12 through explicit uncertainty statements. The system autonomously generates structured warnings that integrate causal mechanisms, spatiotemporal evolution, quantitative evidence, regulatory references, and confidence statements--enhancing professional depth, logical rigor, and scientific soundness, and advancing meteorological services toward proactive perception, explainable decision-making, and intelligent agency.

</details>


### [109] [Kernel-Managed Shared Memory for System-Wide Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10144)
*Ryan Lum,Yongfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种内核管理的共享内存抽象，将内存检索、隐私执行和提示注入集中在智能体系统内核中，而非单个智能体。实验表明，该方法在三种模型（GPT-4o、Llama-3.1:8B、Qwen-2.5:7B）和1800次试验中，相比无管理外部内存、标准RAG和无过滤上下文拼接，显著提升了个性化得分（2.4-4.0分/5分制），同时将端到端延迟降低15-61%，大幅减少token使用和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，一个智能体学到的有用上下文往往无法被其他智能体获取，导致个性化能力受限。现有的外部内存后端（如Mem0）将检索和隐私执行留给单个智能体，缺乏统一管理；标准RAG和无过滤上下文拼接则存在成本高、延迟大或隐私风险。需要一种系统级抽象，由内核统一管理内存的写入、检索、隐私和注入，以实现高效、安全的跨智能体个性化。

Method: 设计并实现了内核管理的共享内存（kernel-managed shared memory）抽象：专用智能体写入结构化、带标签的内存；智能体系统内核（而非单个智能体）负责检索、隐私执行和提示注入。在AIOS上实现该设计，并与三种基线对比：无管理外部内存后端、标准检索增强注入、无过滤上下文拼接。使用GPT-4o、Llama-3.1:8B、Qwen-2.5:7B三种模型，共1800次试验进行评估。

Result: 相比无管理外部内存，内核管理检索和注入使个性化得分提升2.4-4.0分（5分制），如GPT-4o上档案使用率从1.05升至4.69，所有对比p < 10^-18。相比标准RAG，增益同样大且跨模型一致。相比无过滤上下文拼接，在三模型中两模型性能统计相当，第三模型有小幅模型特定劣势，但提示显著更短：端到端延迟降低15-61%，token使用和推理成本相应减少。

Conclusion: 将内存管理集中在智能体系统内核，而非留给单个智能体，能以极小成本获得无约束上下文的大部分个性化收益。内核管理的共享内存抽象实现了高效、安全、可扩展的跨智能体个性化，是多智能体系统的有效系统级解决方案。

Abstract: AI systems become more useful when they can adapt to the people using them, but in multi-agent systems, useful context learned by one agent often remains unavailable to others. We present kernel-managed shared memory, a system-level abstraction in which specialized agents write structured, tagged memories while the agent-system kernel, not individual agents, governs retrieval, privacy enforcement, and prompt injection. We implement and evaluate this design on AIOS and compare it against three alternatives across three assistant models (GPT-4o, Llama-3.1:8B, Qwen-2.5:7B) and 1,800 total trials. Against an unmanaged external memory backend (Mem0) using identical underlying storage, kernel-managed retrieval and injection improve personalization scores by 2.4-4.0 points on a 5-point scale (e.g., 1.05 to 4.69 profile usage on GPT-4o), with every comparison significant at p < 10^-18. Against standard retrieval-augmented injection, gains are similarly large and consistent across all three models. Against full, unfiltered context concatenation, a soft ceiling on available context rather than on response quality, kernel-managed injection statistically matches performance on two of three models and shows a small, model-specific deficit on the third, while using substantially shorter prompts: end-to-end latency is 15-61% lower across all three models, with corresponding reductions in per-call token usage and inference cost. These results indicate that centralizing memory management in the agent-system kernel, rather than leaving retrieval and privacy enforcement to individual agents, delivers most of the personalization benefit of unconstrained context at a fraction of its cost.

</details>


### [110] [Beyond Surface Imitation: Contrastive Modeling for Reasoning Path Alignment in Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10177)
*Mingbo Yang,Wenqiang Wang,Zhaolu Kang,Peng Chen,Yannan Chen,Sunshang Wang,Yan Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合对比演示建模与自优化能力的多模态上下文学习框架，通过显式对比次优与更优响应及推理路径，并引入响应条件检索和轻量级对齐控制器，显著提升了MLLM在复杂多模态任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态ICL方法依赖表层模仿，难以使MLLM的响应与给定多模态输入所需的推理路径对齐，尤其在复杂任务中限制了性能提升。

Method: 提出对比演示建模：将每个演示重构为同一输入下次优响应与更优响应的显式对比，并附带推理路径；引入响应条件检索机制，根据当前响应选择相关推理路径的演示；使用轻量级对齐控制器预测响应质量并决定是否需进一步优化。

Result: 在三类多模态任务上实验表明，该框架持续提升MLLM性能，在视觉问答（VQA）任务上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 通过对比演示建模和自优化机制，该框架有效引导MLLM超越表层模仿，显式学习推理路径，为复杂多模态任务的上下文学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is widely used in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and achieves strong performance across a wide range of multimodal tasks. However, existing multimodal ICL methods often rely on surface level imitation of in-context demonstrations, making it difficult for MLLMs to align their responses with the reasoning path required by the given multimodal input. This limitation becomes more pronounced in complex multimodal tasks, thereby restricting further improvements in MLLM performance. To address this issue, we propose a new multimodal ICL framework that combines contrastive demonstration modeling with the self-refinement capability of MLLMs. Specifically, our framework reformulates each demonstration by explicitly contrasting a suboptimal response with a better response under the same input, together with a reasoning path that reveals how the response should be refined. This contrastive formulation makes the reasoning path toward the desired response more explicit and guides the MLLM beyond superficial imitation. Furthermore, because effective refinement depends on the current response, we introduce a response-conditioned retrieval mechanism to select demonstrations whose reasoning paths are more relevant to the current response. In addition, we use a lightweight alignment controller to predict response quality and determine whether further refinement is needed. Experiments on three types of multimodal tasks show that the proposed framework consistently improves MLLM performance, with particularly notable gains on visual question answering (VQA).

</details>


### [111] [Why Sample What You Can Enumerate? Exact Policy Optimization for Genomic Tool Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10221)
*Haoyue Liu,Xiaoyu Ma,Ye Chen,Zhichao Wang,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出在工具子集空间可枚举的专业科学场景中，GRPO 存在结构性错配，提出 FGPO 通过穷尽评分所有工具子集并预计算奖励表，实现精确动作期望优化，显著提升性能并减少工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专业科学领域（如基因组学推理），工具子集空间虽组合但可枚举，GRPO 依赖采样估计动作期望，随着策略收敛导致采样奖励碰撞、群组归一化优势消失，训练信号退化（无奖励信号问题比例从 0.2% 升至 20.8%），需一种能利用完整动作空间且移除冻结推理器调用的方法。

Method: 提出 FGPO（Full-Group Policy Optimization）：(1) 对每个工具子集进行穷尽评分，优化精确的动作期望，使每次更新覆盖完整动作空间；(2) 将每个问题-子集对的奖励预计算为穷尽表，彻底从训练循环中移除冻结推理器的奖励评估调用。

Result: 在五个冻结推理器和三个基因组基准上共 15 个设置中，FGPO 全部优于 GRPO，平均提升 6.75 分，最高 14.20 分；标准按需 GRPO 需 2.4 倍的冻结推理器奖励评估次数；在 GenomeQA 上，FGPO 将每问题平均调用工具数从 2.36 降至 1.40。

Conclusion: FGPO 通过穷尽动作空间评分和预计算奖励表，解决了 GRPO 在可枚举工具子集空间中的结构性错配，实现了更高效、更精确的策略优化，显著提升性能并降低计算开销与工具调用数量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning over a frozen reasoner has become a common recipe for teaching a policy which external tools to invoke. We show that this recipe becomes structurally mismatched in specialist scientific settings where the complete tool-subset space is enumerable. There, a small set of recurring computational capabilities covers the domain, so the space of tool subsets is combinatorial yet small enough to enumerate, and GRPO still estimates an action expectation from a handful of sampled rollouts. Worse, the approximation degrades as training succeeds: as the policy concentrates on preferred subsets it resamples them, sampled rewards collide, and the group-normalized advantage vanishes. On genomic reasoning the fraction of questions yielding no reward signal rises from 0.2% under a uniform reference policy to 20.8% after GRPO training. As a remedy, we introduce FGPO (Full-Group Policy Optimization), which (1) scores every tool subset and optimizes the exact action expectation, so each update sees the complete action space, and (2) precomputes the reward of each question--subset pair into an exhaustive table, removing frozen-reasoner calls from the training loop entirely. Across five frozen reasoners and three genomic benchmarks, FGPO outperforms GRPO in all 15 settings by 6.75 points on average and up to 14.20, while a standard on-demand GRPO schedule would require 2.4 times as many frozen-reasoner reward evaluations and, on GenomeQA, FGPO cuts invoked tools per question from 2.36 to 1.40.

</details>


### [112] [What Should an Agent Forget? Separating What Is Stored from What Is Used](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10263)
*Yuhang Li,Yuchen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RD-Forget，一个无需训练的持久语言智能体框架，通过分离存储与使用，利用保留的源档案和查询条件记忆视图，解决事实更新与历史查询并存的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久语言智能体需要跨时间保持可用的存储经验，但每个回答需要针对特定问题的证据。被取代的事实可能误导当前状态回答，却对历史查询至关重要。现有方法难以同时兼顾事实更新与历史保留。

Method: RD-Forget 包含三大核心组件：1) 保留的源档案存储所有观察；2) 查询条件记忆视图控制证据对当前回答的影响；3) 冻结的语言模型策展人提取相关证据、将事实分组到语义槽位并保留多跳推理所需关系。同槽位替换链接抑制当前语境下的被取代值，意图感知检索使早期证据再次可用。率失真公式指导在记忆预算内构建回答时视图。

Result: 实验涵盖对话记忆、知识更新、事实巩固、长上下文推理和个性化五大任务。准确回答与查询相关证据构建及对过时替代方案的控制相关。无遗忘或无查询条件的配置得分缺失最大；槽位分组、历史访问和关系保留提供互补功能。

Conclusion: 保留历史同时选择性控制其使用，为适应不断变化的事实和未来问题提供了实用途径。RD-Forget 通过训练自由的架构实现了存储与使用的解耦，有效平衡了当前状态准确性与历史查询需求。

Abstract: Persistent language agents need stored experience to remain available across time, while each answer requires evidence suited to a particular question. A superseded fact can mislead a current-state answer and still be essential for a historical query. We present RD-Forget, a training-free framework that separates what an agent stores from what it uses. A retained source archive preserves observations, and a query-conditioned memory view controls their influence on the current answer. A frozen language-model curator extracts relevant evidence, groups facts into semantic slots, and preserves the relations needed for multi-hop reasoning. Same-slot replacement links suppress superseded values in current-state contexts, while intent-aware retrieval makes earlier evidence eligible again. A rate-distortion formulation guides construction of the answer-time view within a memory budget. Experiments span conversational memory, knowledge updating, fact consolidation, long-context reasoning, and personalization under a shared answering pipeline. The results associate accurate answers with both query-relevant evidence construction and control over obsolete alternatives. Configurations without forgetting or query conditioning have the largest score deficits, while slot grouping, historical access, and relation preservation contribute complementary functions. Retaining history while selectively controlling its use offers a practical way to accommodate changing facts and future questions.

</details>


### [113] [TRACE: Training Reasoning Agents for Causal Exploration with Synthesized Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10315)
*Rui Sun,Zhan Shi,Bing He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 TRACE，一个基于模拟器的数字广告诊断环境，通过注入已知干预生成可验证奖励，训练 RL 代理在诊断推理任务上超越更大规模的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诊断推理缺乏数学/代码任务中廉价的客观验证手段；作者通过模拟器注入已知干预作为预言机标签，将验证不对称性转化为可工程化的训练信号。

Method: 构建 TRACE 环境（12 种根因、细粒度段归因），代理使用 Python/SQL 调查；先监督微调 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，再用模拟器合成的奖励进行强化学习。

Result: 在 235 个保留测试集上，Claude Opus 5 达到 0.686 FullAttr@1；Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 经 SFT 从 0.159 提升至 0.637，经 RL 进一步提升至 0.757，超越所有基线（包括更大模型），且工具调用显著减少。

Conclusion: 可扩展的客观训练信号比单纯扩大模型规模更关键；基于模拟的验证使原本模糊的诊断推理任务适合大规模强化学习。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has advanced language-model reasoning in domains such as mathematics and code, where objective answers are inexpensive to check. Diagnostic reasoning over complex data lacks this advantage: establishing the true cause of an anomaly often requires costly expert investigation and may remain ambiguous after the fact. We ask whether this asymmetry of verification can instead be engineered. We sample an intervention, inject it into a controlled simulator, and generate the observations it would produce. The hidden intervention provides an oracle label and objective reward, while the agent must still investigate noisy, confounded, and distributed evidence.
  We instantiate this approach in TRACE, a digital-advertising diagnostic environment with 12 root causes and fine-grained segment attribution. Agents investigate each episode using Python and SQL and must identify both the root cause and, when applicable, the affected segment assignment. On a held-out 235-episode test set, the strongest prompted baseline, Claude Opus 5, reaches 0.686 FullAttr@1. Supervised fine-tuning raises Qwen3.5-35B-A3B from 0.159 to 0.637, and subsequent RL with synthesized rewards reaches 0.757, outperforming all evaluated prompted baselines, including frontier closed-source models and a prompted Qwen3.5-122B-A10B model. The resulting policy also uses substantially fewer tool calls than the prompted 35B base. These results provide evidence that access to a scalable, objective training signal can be a more important constraint than model scale alone. More broadly, simulation-based verification can make otherwise ambiguous diagnostic reasoning tasks amenable to scalable reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [114] [From Symbolic Perception to Logical Deduction: A Framework for Guiding Language Models in Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10335)
*Weichen Dai,Rafael Medeiros Cabral,Ziyi Shou,Yan Cao,Xin Shen,Dongcai Lu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种纯大语言模型(LLM)框架，通过集成几何视觉解析器和符号求解器，在平面几何问题上达到与最先进的大型多模态模型(LMMs)相当的性能，同时提供可解释的类人推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面几何需要融合视觉感知与数学推理。虽然LMMs天然处理视觉-语言输入，但计算量大且不透明。作者旨在证明：配备专用模块的纯LLM可以媲美最先进的LMMs，解决复杂几何问题。

Method: 框架包含两个核心模块：(1)几何视觉解析器，将几何图形翻译为符号形式；(2)符号求解器，执行形式化演绎推理。这种设计缓解了幻觉问题，促进可解释推理。为严格评估，作者从2025年中考真题中构建了具有数据新颖性的基准测试，考察更深层的演绎能力。

Result: 实验表明，该方法性能与Gemini 2.5 Pro相当，同时提供更清晰、类人的解题过程。

Conclusion: 纯LLM结合专用几何模块能有效处理复杂几何问题，实现可解释的推理，性能媲美最先进的LMMs，为几何问题求解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Plane geometry remains a significant challenge in AI, requiring the integration of visual perception and mathematical reasoning. While Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) naturally handle visuo-linguistic inputs, they are often computationally intensive and opaque. We demonstrate that a pure Large Language Model (LLM), when equipped with specialized modules, can rival state-of-the-art LMMs on complex geometry problems. Our framework integrates a Geometric Vision Parser, which translates diagrams into symbolic form, with a Symbolic Solver that performs formal deductions, thereby mitigating hallucinations and promoting interpretable reasoning. To enable rigorous evaluation, we curate a benchmark of challenging problems from the 2025 Chinese Zhongkao examinations, ensuring data novelty and testing deeper deductive skills. Experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves performance comparable to Gemini 2.5 Pro while delivering clearer, human-like solutions.

</details>


### [115] [Cyber-Financial Contagion: Modeling the Propagation of an AI Vendor Compromise Through the Banking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10350)
*Alex Leytes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI供应商集中风险如何通过四层异构网络传播引发类金融危机，提出CFC-Prop模型模拟传播动态，并训练CFC-GNN预警模型，实验显示AUROC 0.82、AUPRC 0.60，指出AI供应商网络集中度是一级金融稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行系统高度依赖少数共享AI供应商进行反欺诈、信贷决策、反洗钱、客户分析等，单一供应商被攻破可能通过运营、信息、金融链路级联放大，引发类似经典银行业危机的损失，现有缺乏量化工具评估此类系统性风险。

Method: 构建四层异构网络（AI供应商、金融机构、银行间敞口、客户账户），提出随机流行病-清算模型CFC-Prop模拟级联传播；基于供应商侧事件遥测与图结构训练图神经网络预警模型CFC-GNN识别高级联风险供应商；在合成数据集（60供应商、220银行、~2500服务边、1400银行间敞口）上验证。

Result: CFC-Prop能复现重尾损失分布与对补丁延迟的敏感依赖，符合既往网络-金融实证；CFC-GNN在四基线上达到AUROC 0.82、AUPRC 0.60且校准误差有界；发布完整代码、合成数据与复现脚本。

Conclusion: AI供应商的网络集中度构成一级金融稳定问题，监管机构可利用本文提供的量化工具进行系统性风险推演与早期预警。

Abstract: The banking system now depends on a small set of shared artificial intelligence vendors for fraud screening, credit decisioning, anti-money-laundering triage, customer analytics, and internal decision support. This paper studies how a compromise inside one of those vendors can propagate along a chain of operational, informational, and financial linkages until it triggers losses that look, from the outside, like a classical banking crisis. We build a four-layer heterogeneous network that couples AI vendors, financial institutions, interbank exposures, and customer accounts, and we propose CFC-Prop, a stochastic epidemic-and-clearing model that runs on that network. On a synthetic dataset with 60 vendors, 220 banks, roughly 2,500 vendor-bank service edges, and 1,400 interbank exposures, CFC-Prop reproduces the heavy-tailed loss distributions and the sharp dependence on patch latency that are consistent with prior cyber-financial evidence. We also train an early-warning model, CFC-GNN, that uses vendor-side incident telemetry and graph structure to flag high-cascade-risk vendors before impact. Across four baselines the proposed model reaches AUROC 0.82 and AUPRC 0.60 while keeping calibration errors bounded. We release the full code, synthetic data, and reproducible scripts. The results argue that cyber concentration among AI vendors is a first-order financial-stability problem and give supervisors a concrete quantitative tool for reasoning about it.

</details>


### [116] [Fortunate Recall: Ontology-Driven Memory Lifecycle Management for Persistent Coherence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10413)
*Ansuman Mullick,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Fortunate Recall (FR)，一种可组合的策略层，通过将个人事实分类为 10+1 行为本体并应用类别特定的生命周期策略（差分时间衰减、槽位键取代、事件时间有效性、类别感知检索路由），解决了 LLM 记忆系统中记忆无界增长与检索精度下降的问题。FR-Bank 实现显著优于现有基线，在 LifecycleBench 达到 76.9% 通过率，LongMemEval-S 75.2%，并将 Mem0 的幻觉率从 45.1% 降至 22.4%（已回答查询），同时提升正确回答比例。消融实验表明通用生命周期元数据贡献正确性优势，行为本体提供校准优势，将幻觉减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 记忆系统对所有个人事实一视同仁，导致记忆存储无界增长、检索精度退化。核心挑战在于生命周期管理：基于事实的行为类型决定哪些记忆应持久化、哪些应被替换以及替换速率。

Method: 提出 Fortunate Recall (FR) 策略层：(1) 将个人事实分类为 10+1 行为本体；(2) 基于 LLM 提取的元数据，为每类应用确定性的类别特定生命周期策略：差分时间衰减、槽位键取代、事件时间有效性、类别感知检索路由。实现了与基础设施无关的 FR-Bank。

Result: FR-Bank 在新基准 LifecycleBench (516 题) 达到 76.9% 通过率，超越 Mem0、A-MEM、Memory-R1、MemoryOS (61%-70.5%)；LongMemEval-S 75.2% 显示无额外检索开销。消融实验：用三个通用生命周期原语替换类型层，正确性无显著变化 (-1.7pp, 95% CI [-6.0, +2.7])，表明通用元数据提供正确性优势，行为本体提供校准优势，将下游幻觉减半 (12.0% vs 24.2%, p<0.001)。端到端：FR-Bank 将 Mem0 幻觉从 45.1% 降至 22.4%（已回答）和 32.2% 降至 13.0%（所有查询），正确回答率从 18.6% 提升至 31.2%，在 Kimi K2.5 上复现。迁移至 BEAM 基准：46.8% vs 32.9%，本体优势集中在矛盾解决，七个策略簇附近饱和。

Conclusion: 行为本体驱动的类别特定生命周期策略能有效管理记忆存储与检索，通用生命周期元数据提供正确性基础，本体提供校准显著降低幻觉。FR 为 LLM 记忆系统提供了可迁移、可解释的生命周期管理解决方案，相关本体、基准与代码已开源。

Abstract: Current LLM memory systems treat all personal facts identically, so stores grow without bound while retrieval precision degrades. The core challenge is lifecycle management: which memories should persist, which should be replaced, and at what rate, conditioned on the behavioral type of each fact. Fortunate Recall (FR) is a composable policy layer that classifies personal facts into a 10+1 behavioral ontology and applies category-specific lifecycle policies (differential temporal decay, slot-key supersession, event-time validity, and category-aware retrieval routing) as deterministic functions over LLM-extracted metadata. FR-Bank, our infrastructure-independent implementation, reaches a 76.9% pass rate on LifecycleBench, a new 516-question temporal-disambiguation benchmark, ahead of Mem0, A-MEM, Memory-R1, and MemoryOS (61% to 70.5%), and 75.2% on the full LongMemEval-S under the canonical Wu et al. judge protocol, so lifecycle policies impose no measurable cost on standard retrieval. A pre-registered ablation locates the gains: replacing the typed layer with three generic lifecycle primitives leaves correctness statistically unchanged (-1.7pp, 95% CI [-6.0, +2.7]), so the generic lifecycle metadata carries the correctness advantage, while the behavioral ontology carries calibration, halving downstream confabulation (12.0% vs 24.2%, p<0.001). End-to-end, FR-Bank cuts confabulation from Mem0's 45.1% to 22.4% over answered queries and from 32.2% to 13.0% over all queries while answering more of them correctly (31.2% vs 18.6%); the ranking replicates on the open-weight Kimi K2.5. The decomposition transfers to BEAM, an independently built benchmark: 46.8% correct vs Mem0's 32.9% over 280 questions, with the ontology's benefit concentrated in contradiction resolution and saturating near seven policy clusters. The ontology, benchmark, and code are released.

</details>


### [117] [ConvMem: Convolutional Memory for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10441)
*Hongming Zhang,Zhaozhen Gu,Fengshuo Bai,Ming Hao,Qingyang Zhang,Yuanyuan Wang,Shiyang Tang,Yanna Wang,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConvMem是一种无需训练、高度可并行化的框架，将长上下文推理重构为分层卷积操作。它将带有特定查询提示的LLM视为卷积核，通过配置步长、跳跃连接和多核卷积，将推理路径从线性链缩短为对数树，解决了序列化方法（如MemAgent）的高延迟和RL训练过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）因固定上下文限制难以处理超长上下文。现有序列化方法（如MemAgent）通过分段读取和迭代更新固定大小记忆来扩展有效上下文，但存在高延迟、需要昂贵RL训练且易在特定数据集上过拟合的缺陷。

Method: ConvMem将长上下文推理重构为分层卷积：(1) 将带查询提示的LLM作为卷积核，对文本片段进行分层摘要，将推理路径从线性链转为对数树；(2) 引入可配置步长和跳跃连接，保证证据的稳健捕获与传播；(3) 采用多核卷积将复杂查询解耦为独立语义通道；(4) 设计无需训练，支持跨文本片段和推理线程的大规模并行化。

Result: 在RULER-HotpotQA和RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA基准上，ConvMem超越无训练基线，且避免了RL训练模型在分布外任务上常见的参数先验过拟合风险。

Conclusion: ConvMem提供了一种无训练、高并行的长上下文推理新范式，通过分层卷积架构有效缓解误差累积，在性能和泛化性上均优于现有序列化和RL训练方法。}

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities, they often struggle with extremely long contexts due to fixed context limits. To address this, sequential approaches like MemAgent extend the effective context by reading text in segments and iteratively updating a fixed-size memory. However, this sequential paradigm suffers from high latency and requires costly reinforcement learning (RL) training, which can lead to overfitting on specific datasets. To overcome these limitations, we propose ConvMem, a training-free, highly parallelizable framework that reformulates long-context reasoning as a hierarchical convolution. Inspired by CNNs, ConvMem treats an LLM prompted with a specific query as a convolutional kernel. This kernel summarizes text segments hierarchically, shortening the reasoning path from a linear chain into a logarithmic tree. Specifically, ConvMem integrates \textit{Configurable Strides} and \textit{Skip Connections} to ensure robust evidence capture and propagation, while employing \textit{Multi-Kernel Convolution} to decompose complex queries into disentangled semantic channels. This design not only mitigates error accumulation but also enables massive parallelization across both text segments and reasoning threads. Experiments on RULER-HotpotQA and RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA demonstrate that ConvMem outperforms training-free baselines and avoids the risk of overfitting to parametric priors often observed in RL-trained models on out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [118] [JarvisGUI: Towards Cross-Device GUI Agents with Dynamic Task Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10451)
*Zixiang Chen,Yuheng Lu,Zihao Cheng,Zeming Liu,Jizeng Bai,Ziye Huang,Zhiyin Lin,Zihan Li,Yuhang Guo,Yunhong Wang,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出JarvisGUI，一个跨设备GUI代理动态基准，揭示现有最先进开源GUI代理在跨平台工作流中的关键能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界GUI使用涉及跨多设备、多平台的工作流，需传输中间结果、维护共享状态、协调异构环境；但现有基准仅评估单设备静态任务，导致对代理真实就绪度的过度乐观评估。

Method: 将GUI任务形式化为轻量类型系统下的输入-输出变换，自动合成多步跨设备工作流，在涵盖Android、Windows、Ubuntu的虚拟环境中统一动态评估代理表现。

Result: 最先进开源GUI代理在状态迁移感知、跨平台上下文推理、长时程依赖管理三方面均表现挣扎，暴露出现有基准不可见的关键能力鸿沟。

Conclusion: 跨设备协作能力是GUI代理走向实用的关键缺口，JarvisGUI为该方向提供了系统性评测框架，推动未来研究聚焦真实世界工作流挑战。

Abstract: Real-world GUI usage frequently involves workflows that span multiple devices and platforms, requiring the transfer of intermediate results, maintenance of shared state, and coordination across heterogeneous environments. However, existing GUI benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate agents on single-device, statically defined tasks, thus leaving such cross-device capabilities largely unexamined, resulting in an overly optimistic assessment of agents' readiness for real-world usage. We introduce JarvisGUI, a dynamic benchmark that evaluates GUI agents on cross-device workflows requiring coordinated interaction across heterogeneous platforms, including Android, Windows, and Ubuntu. Specifically, JarvisGUI formulates GUI tasks as input-output transformations under a lightweight type system, which allows us to automatically compose multi-step, cross-device workflows and dynamically evaluate agent performance within a unified framework. By evaluating agents in virtual environments spanning multiple operating systems, JarvisGUI reveals that state-of-the-art open-source GUI agents struggle with the state-transfer awareness, cross-platform contextual reasoning, and long-horizon dependency management required for real-world workflows, exposing a critical capability gap invisible to existing benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Spectral origin of the topological gap exponent d + η: mechanism, kernel, decomposition, and scope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09159)
*Matthew Loftus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究拓扑间隙Δ的标度行为，通过谱积分I(α)推导Δ~L^{d+η}，分解为体积因子I_0和异常维度因子I_shape，发现机制要求d<2+η（仅限d=2物理系统），d=3时需密度归一化。Potts q=4模拟中α_opt在[-0.75,-0.5]区间，与Ising的-2η一致但与q=4的-1不一致，需更大L验证。逐构型相关性主要源于磁化强度，去除后相关性消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解拓扑间隙Δ在临界点云中的标度规律，揭示其与谱积分、磁化强度及异常维度的关系，解释不同维度下密度归一化的必要性，验证Potts模型与Ising模型的关联。

Method: 理论推导谱积分I(α)的标度行为及分解；数值模拟Potts q=4模型（L=32-256）进行α扫描；逐构型分析Δ与I(-0.5)的相关性及磁化强度的影响；协方差修正分析超缩放乘积；启发式论证拓扑数据分析核的k依赖性。

Result: Δ~L^{d+η}；I(α)在IR主导时标度为L^{2-α-η}，导致I(-2η)~L^{d+η}；分解I(-2η)=I_0·I_shape，I_0~N(1-m^2)~L^d，I_shape~L^η；机制要求d<2+η，仅d=2物理系统满足，d=3为UV主导需密度归一化；Potts q=4的α_opt∈[-0.75,-0.5]，与-2η_Ising一致，与-2η_{q=4}=-1不一致（待L≥1024验证）；⟨m^2·I(-2η)⟩由r(m^2,I)≈-0.98主导；逐构型Δ与I(-0.5)相关性R^2=0.91，去除|M|后R^2≈0；逐构型支持I_0 Parseval恒等式，但形状因子|k|^{-2η}需系团L标度确立。

Conclusion: 拓扑间隙标度由体积因子和异常维度因子决定，维度限制d<2+η解释了二维系统的特殊性；Potts q=4表现出Ising类普适性迹象但需更大规模验证；逐构型相关性本质是磁化强度驱动，拓扑核的k依赖仅在临界点出现；理论框架将持久同调与统计物理关联。

Abstract: The topological gap $Δ$ -- the excess $H_1$ total persistence of a critical point cloud over a density-matched null -- scales as $Δ\sim L^{d+η}$. We derive this analytically: the spectral integral $I(α) = \sum_{k\neq 0} S_{\mathrm{conn}}(k)\,|k|^α$ scales as $L^{2-α-η}$ when IR-dominated, giving $I(-2η) \sim L^{d+η}$. The decomposition $I(-2η) = I_0 \cdot I_{\mathrm{shape}}$ separates volume ($I_0 \propto N(1-m^2) \sim L^d$) from anomalous dimension ($I_{\mathrm{shape}} \sim L^η$); the volume factor accounts for the magnetization-driven per-configuration variance of $Δ$. We prove the mechanism requires $d < 2 + η$ (IR dominance), confining it to $d = 2$ for physical systems; in $d = 3$ the spectral integral is UV-dominated, explaining why density normalization is needed. An $α$-sweep for Potts $q = 4$ at $L = 32$--$256$ finds $α_{\mathrm{opt}}$ in $[-0.75, -0.5]$, consistent with $-2η_{\mathrm{Ising}}$ and inconsistent with $-2η_{q=4} = -1$; we flag this as tentative pending $L \geq 1024$ confirmation. The $\langle m^2 \cdot I(-2η)\rangle$ hyperscaling product is dominated by the correlation $r(m^2, I) \approx -0.98$ via the shared $I_0$ amplitude, so we report it as a covariance-correction analysis. Under a heuristic argument extending Divol--Polonik to inhomogeneous Poisson intensities, the bare PH kernel is flat; the effective kernel acquires $k$-dependence only at criticality. The per-configuration agreement between $Δ$ and $I(-0.5)$ is primarily a magnetization correlation: $R^2 = 0.91$ at $L = 256$ collapses to $R^2 \approx 0$ once $|M|$ is partialed out. Per-configuration evidence corroborates the $I_0$ Parseval identity but not the $|k|^{-2η}$ shape factor; the latter is established by ensemble $L$-scaling.

</details>


### [120] [Physics-informed neural networks by Gradient-Guided Gaussian Adaptive Sampling (3GAS-PINNs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09162)
*Yousen Wang,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 3GAS-PINNs 通过梯度引导的高斯自适应采样解决传统 PINNs 在非线性问题中的收敛慢、梯度不平衡及无法捕捉激波等间歇结构的问题，在三个基准 PDE 上实现精度最高提升 14 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 PINNs 采用固定损失权重和均匀配点分布，难以适应解场演变的复杂性，导致在强非线性、高梯度问题（如激波、孤立子）中表现不佳，存在收敛慢、梯度不平衡、局部结构分辨率不足等缺陷。

Method: 提出 3GAS-PINNs，融合均匀概率分布与基于解空间梯度的高斯平滑概率分布，自适应调整配点密度：全局维持约束满足，局部聚焦高梯度区域，从而精准捕捉间歇性结构。

Result: 在一维强迫 Burgers 方程、KdV 方程、非线性薛定谔方程三个基准测试中，3GAS-PINNs 显著提升间歇区域物理一致性，数值模拟精度较基线 PINNs 最高提升 14 倍。

Conclusion: 梯度引导的高斯自适应采样有效缓解 PINNs 在强非线性、高梯度问题中的固有缺陷，为求解含间歇结构的偏微分方程提供了高效、免网格的新范式。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a mesh-free framework for solving partial differential equations, yet their performance in nonlinear problems is often limited by slow convergence, gradient imbalance, and insufficient resolution to capture localized intermittent structures such as shock waves[1]. These issues arise primarily from the use of fixed weights of loss and uniform collocation point distributions, which cannot adapt to the evolving complexity of the solution field during training. To address these challenges, Gradient-Guided Gaussian Adaptive Sampling Physics-Informed Neural Networks (3GAS-PINNs) is proposed in this paper, which combines uniform probability distribution and Gaussian-smoothed probability distribution derived from the spatial gradients of solution, to maintain global constraint satisfaction as well as concentrating collocation points in regions of high gradient. Thus, intermittency structures like shock wave and solitons can be accurately captured. The method is evaluated on three benchmark nonlinear problems, including one-dimensional forced Burgers equation, Korteweg-de Vries (KdV) equation and nonlinear Schrodinger equation, all of which exhibit steep gradients or strong nonlinearity. In comparison with baseline PINNs, 3GAS-PINNs can effectively promote the physical consistency in intermittent regions. The accuracy of the numerical simulation can be improved by a factor of up to 14.

</details>


### [121] [World-Time Compute with Verified Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09163)
*James Schwoebel,Ingrida Semenec,Jenia Rousseva,Marcos Ortiz,Collin Overbay,Christopher Klaus,Anderson Edmond,Manish Bhatt,Rome Thorstenson,Jessica Tsai,Martin G. Frasch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出通过代码定义的世界模型生成精确标注的轨迹数据，利用“世界时间计算”在训练阶段微调LLM，显著提升小模型在未见世界中的泛化能力，验证标签精确性而非任务多样性是关键，并在ARC-AGI、List Functions、CLRS等真实基准上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在领域泛化需要大量真实标注样本，但大多数领域缺乏此类数据。研究旨在通过可执行、可验证的代码世界模型廉价制造精确标注轨迹，解决数据稀缺问题。

Method: 将领域动态编写为代码，一个模板实例化为多个世界模型（符号状态上的可执行程序），生成无限精确标注轨迹。在遍历多个此类世界的轨迹上微调LLM，称为“世界时间计算”（训练时的测试时计算类比）。使用OpenWorld零依赖框架编写和服务世界。

Result: 在合成世界族上，微调后LLM泛化到未训练世界，增益最大出现在能力最稀缺处：0.5B模型提升29分；最大模型增益在噪声范围内（饱和）。标签可信因世界为验证代码：20步展开精确，10倍OOD探测100%准确，而LLP和MLP逐步预测误差累积崩溃。对比域随机化，每个世界独立编写验证；损坏标签对照实验证明标签精确性驱动增益而非任务多样性。真实基准（ARC-AGI网格、List Functions、CLRS）同理，作为每世界测试时训练。List Functions跨世界更难设定：128个不相交世界训练的适配器在保留世界达40%，损坏标签对照仅6%（+34分，CI[29,39]）。增益为饱和规律非定律：少步推理、小/弱模型增益大，长链、感知诱导任务、饱和任务衰减；无共享技能跨任务迁移弱。

Conclusion: 代码定义的世界模型提供精确标注轨迹，通过世界时间计算微调能显著提升小模型跨世界泛化，标签精确性是核心驱动因素。该方法在符号状态任务有效，像素原生领域仍需学习模型。所有代码、配方、手稿可从单一仓库再生。

Abstract: LLMs generalize across a domain only after seeing many real, labeled examples, which most domains lack. We study a way to manufacture it cheaply. When a domain's dynamics can be written as code, one template instantiates into many world models: executable, verifiable programs over symbolic state, each an inexhaustible source of exactly-labeled trajectories. Fine-tuning an LLM on trajectories through many such worlds, which we call world-time compute, a training-time analogue of test-time compute, lifts generalization to held-out worlds it never trained on (synthesized world families). Gains are largest where capability is scarcest: +29 points at 0.5B; the largest model's lift is within noise, consistent with saturation. Labels can be trusted because the worlds are verified code: synthesized-then-checked dynamics are exact over 20-step rollouts and answer 10x out-of-distribution probes exactly (100%), whereas per-step LLM and MLP predictors compound error and collapse. Unlike domain randomization, each world is independently authored and verified; a corrupted-label control shows label exactness, not task variety, drives the gains. On real benchmarks (ARC-AGI grids, List Functions, CLRS) the same lever holds as per-world test-time training. On List Functions the harder cross-world form holds: one adapter trained on 128 disjoint worlds reaches 40% on held-out worlds versus 6% for a corrupted-label control (+34 points, CI [29, 39]). The gain is a saturating regularity, not a law: largest for few-step reasoning and small/weak models, fading for long chains, perception-induced tasks, and saturated tasks; cross-task transfer is weak without shared skill. Worlds are authored and served by OpenWorld, a zero-dependency framework (companion paper). Scope: symbolic state; pixel-native domains remain territory of learned models. All code, recipes, and this manuscript regenerate from one repository.

</details>


### [122] [Scaling Post-Training Ternarisation to Qwen3-8B Capability Retention, Reproduction, Lossless Packing, and Packed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09240)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将激进的后训练量化流程从 Qwen3-4B 扩展到 Qwen3-8B，采用 KOTMS 旋转、E2M-ATQ 自适应三值化和 GPTQ 风格误差补偿（仅权重 A16 配置）。8B 模型在三语料库上的困惑度比率达 1.361 倍，零样本任务准确率 64.6%（FP16 为 72.4%），机会校正保持率 78.5%。打包检查点 8.24 GiB 保持困惑度精度，直接打包执行达 15.52 tokens/s（7.35 GiB），但初步打包 GEMV 仍慢于 FP16 cuBLAS。结论：模型规模提升对激进后训练离散化的鲁棒性，实际序列化已解决，直接执行可行，但更广泛的种子、校准分布和内核优化仍待探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低比特语言模型虽能降低存储和内存流量，但“1.58-bit”等标称标签无法指明部署表示及其执行成本。因此需要研究激进后训练转换流程的规模化效果，明确实际部署表示与执行代价。

Method: 采用 KOTMS 旋转、E2M-ATQ 自适应三值化、GPTQ 风格误差补偿的仅权重 A16 配置。贡献在于端到端的规模化表征：外部复现门控、匹配 4B/8B 能力分析、跨语料库困惑度、有效比特核算、无损晶格感知打包及直接打包执行。

Result: 8B 模型三语料库困惑度比率 1.361 倍（WikiText-2: 1.318x, C4: 1.393x, PTB: 1.371x）。八个零样本任务 n=500 时平均准确率 64.6% 对比 FP16 72.4%，机会校正保持率 78.5%，绝对代价 7.8 点。匹配 4B 运行保持 69.6%，8B 优势 8.9 点。打包检查点 8.24 GiB 保持困惑度测量精度。直接打包执行 15.52 tokens/s（7.35 GiB），初步打包 GEMV 慢于 FP16 cuBLAS。

Conclusion: 验证了规模化基线：模型规模增强对激进后训练离散化的鲁棒性，实际序列化问题已针对测量工件解决，直接执行可行。更广泛的种子、校准分布和内核优化仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models promise reductions in storage and memory traffic, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not specify the deployed representation or its execution cost. We study a scale-up of an aggressive post-training conversion pipeline from Qwen3-4B to Qwen3-8B.
  The conversion uses KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ adaptive ternarisation, and GPTQ-style error compensation in a weight-only A16 configuration. We do not claim these algorithms as new. Our contribution is the end-to-end scale-up characterisation: an external reproduction gate, matched 4B/8B capability analysis, cross-corpus perplexity, effective-bit accounting, lossless lattice-aware packing, and direct packed execution.
  The 8B model reaches a three-corpus perplexity ratio of 1.361x, with WikiText-2, C4, and PTB ratios of 1.318x, 1.393x, and 1.371x. On eight zero-shot tasks at n = 500, mean accuracy is 64.6% versus 72.4% for FP16, corresponding to 78.5% chance-corrected retention and a 7.8-point absolute cost. The matched 4B run retains 69.6%, yielding an 8.9-point 8B advantage.
  The packed checkpoint is 8.24 GiB and preserves the recorded perplexity to measurement precision. Direct packed execution reaches 15.52 tokens/s in 7.35 GiB, while a preliminary packed GEMV remains slower than FP16 cuBLAS. The result is a validated scale-up baseline: model size improves robustness to aggressive post-training discretisation, actual serialisation is solved for the measured artefact, and direct execution is feasible, while broader seeds, calibration distributions, and kernel optimisation remain open.

</details>


### [123] [Distribution-Consistent Inference for Dynamic Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09241)
*Dohyeon Kim,Bedionita Soro,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对稀疏混合专家模型（SMoE）在推理时动态减少激活专家数量导致的分布偏移问题，提出层级分布对齐（LDA）方法，通过层级校准统计量在推理时对齐表示，恢复性能损失且保持稀疏推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型多采用固定top-k专家选择策略，推理时动态减少激活专家虽能降低计算量，但会偏离训练时的路由配置，导致SMoE输出的RMS尺度和方差增加，引起表示不匹配，除专家容量损失外进一步导致下游性能下降。

Method: 提出层级分布对齐（LDA），一种轻量级推理时校正方法：利用层级校准统计量（如均值、方差），将减少路由配置下的表示分布对齐至默认全路由配置的分布，仅需预计算校准参数，推理开销极小。

Result: 在多个SMoE大语言模型、基准测试和路由策略上，LDA能恢复因减少路由导致的分布偏移所引起的大部分性能损失，同时保持稀疏推理效率，额外开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: LDA有效解决了推理时动态top-k路由引起的分布偏移问题，为SMoE模型的高效自适应推理提供了实用的即插即用方案，无需重新训练即可在计算预算变化时保持性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have emerged as a powerful paradigm for scaling model capacity while preserving efficient inference in large foundation models. However, most MoE models use a fixed top-$k$ expert selection policy, assigning the same expert budget to every token even when fewer experts may be sufficient. Inference-time dynamic top-$k$ routing can reduce computation without retraining, but existing methods often overlook the distributional shift caused by deviating from the training-time routing configuration. We show that reducing the number of activated experts consistently increases the RMS scale and variance of SMoE outputs, inducing a representation mismatch that contributes to downstream performance degradation in addition to the loss of expert capacity. To address this correctable component, we propose Layer-wise Distribution Alignment (LDA), a lightweight inference-time correction that uses layer-wise calibration statistics to align reduced-routing representations with the default configuration. Across multiple SMoE LLMs, benchmarks, and routing strategies, LDA recovers much of the performance lost induced by the distributional shift under reduced routing while preserving sparse-inference efficiency with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [124] [DiffLUT-Net: Differentiable Training of FPGA LUT Networks with Learnable Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09254)
*Jiaqi Ye,Xinrui Gong,Jingcun Wang,Olga Kondrateva,Bing Li,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffLUT-Net 是一种原生 FPGA 神经网络，通过联合学习 6 输入查找表（LUT）的真值表与稀疏连接，实现从头训练并直接导出可综合 Verilog，在五个基准上取得优于现有方法的精度-资源权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 FPGA 部署流程要么仅加速买累加运算，要么将预训练量化模型转换为 LUT，缺乏针对 FPGA 逻辑资源的原生端到端优化，导致精度与资源利用率难以平衡。

Method: 提出可微 LUT 函数松弛与硬件源选择机制，联合学习每个 6 输入 LUT 的 64 个真值表项及其 6 个输入端口的连接来源；训练后将真值表与连接离散化，剪枝未使用逻辑，直接导出可综合 Verilog。

Result: 在五个基准测试中，DiffLUT-Net 实现了优于现有方法的精度-资源权衡，验证了联合学习 LUT 功能与稀疏连接对紧凑型 FPGA 原生推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 联合优化 LUT 真值表与稀疏互连可生成高效、紧凑的 FPGA 原生网络，为无需预训练模型转换的端到端 FPGA 推理部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) enable efficient neural-network inference, but most deployment flows either accelerate multiply-accumulate operations or convert pretrained quantized models into lookup tables (LUTs). We present DiffLUT-Net, an FPGA-native network connected by six-input LUTs that are trained from scratch. We jointly learn the 64 truth-table entries of a LUT and the source to each of its six input ports using a differentiable LUT function relaxation and hardware source selection. After training, the truth tables and connections are discretized, unused logic can be pruned, and the network is exported directly as synthesizable Verilog. Across five benchmarks, DiffLUT-Net achieves favorable accuracy-resource trade-offs. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly learning LUT functions and sparse connectivity for compact FPGA-native inference. The code is available at https://github.com/TUDa-HWAI/DiffLUT-Network.

</details>


### [125] [Accountable and uncertainty-aware evaluation of sensor-based AI under distribution shift: devices, subjects, and nearly three years underground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09257)
*Benny Platte,Rico Thomanek,Christian Roschke,Marc Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种分阶段、可问责的评估协议，用于解决基于传感器的AI系统在部署条件（设备、人员、时间）变化时性能下降的问题。该协议通过四个累积泛化阶段、分位数评估、机会基准、超出现有范围率和基于5%分位数的决策规则，在两个真实地下矿山的智能手机地磁定位任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于传感器的AI系统很少在训练条件下运行：设备、人员和记录时期的变化都会降低性能，而随机训练-测试划分无法揭示这种退化。现有评估方法缺乏对部署条件变化的系统性考量，且依赖均值指标容易掩盖性能分布的尾部风险。

Method: 提出分阶段可问责评估协议：1) 四个累积泛化阶段分别保留设备、主体和时间；2) 每个阶段通过重复训练的分位数与具有正确类别数量的机会基准对比判断；3) 引入超出现有范围率，暴露向部署中不再存在的类别的静默误导；4) 制定显式决策规则，将发布决策绑定到5%分位数而非均值。在两个真实地下矿山中，使用基于智能手机的循环分类器进行无基础设施地磁定位演示，并在第二站点复现训练阶段。

Result: 在训练后34个月、未知设备世代和保留测量员的条件下重新评估未改变的模型：299次重复训练的5%分位数现有条件精度为0.39，是机会水平的16.5倍；在42个可达位置类别中该数值变化±0.08，是重复运行差异的数倍。重复训练显示均值具有误导性：某双峰配置在均值测试中果断通过，但其5%分位数却比机会水平低一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该评估协议通过分阶段泛化、分位数评估和显式决策规则，有效量化了部署条件变化下的模型鲁棒性和不确定性。实验证明均值指标可能严重掩盖尾部风险，而基于5%分位数的决策规则能提供更可靠的部署保障。该方法为传感器AI系统的实际部署评估提供了严谨框架。

Abstract: Sensor-based AI systems are rarely operated under the conditions under which they were trained: devices, personnel and recording epochs change, and each change degrades performance in ways a random train-test split cannot reveal. We propose a staged, accountable evaluation protocol that treats the evaluation of a deployed model as a measurement with declared reference levels and a quantified uncertainty. Four cumulative generalisation stages hold out devices, subjects and time. Each stage is judged on quantiles of repeated trainings against chance references with the correct class count, an out-of-present-scope rate exposes silent misdirection towards classes that are no longer present in deployment relative to training, and an explicit decision rule ties roll-out decisions not to means but to 5% quantiles. We demonstrate the protocol on infrastructure-free geomagnetic localisation with smartphone-based recurrent classifiers in two real underground mines, including a replication of the scheme's training stages at the second site. Unchanged models are re-evaluated on data recorded 34 months after the training campaign, on a device generation unknown at training time and with a held-out surveyor. The 5% quantile of their present-conditioned precision there is 0.39 over 299 repeated trainings, 16.5 times the chance level; across the composition of the 42 reachable location classes the figure varies by +/-0.08, several times the spread between repeated runs. Repeated trainings of a single configuration show why means mislead: a bimodal configuration passes a mean-based test decisively while its 5% quantile lies more than an order of magnitude below chance.

</details>


### [126] [Literati: Towards Anytime Optimal Shape Generalized Trees via AO*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09299)
*Nakul Upadhya,Eldan Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Literati，首个最优形状泛化树（SGT）诱导算法，通过 AND/OR 图联合优化树结构与形状函数复杂度，并基于 AO* 算法引入次级启发式和轮询策略提升实时性能，在 24 个真实数据集上超越现有树模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树虽具可解释性且在表格数据上表现强劲，但贪婪自顶向下诱导算法往往产生次优且过度复杂的结构。现有最优决策树方法虽通过全局优化缓解此问题，但受限于轴对齐阈值分割，表达力有限，导致树模型过深过复杂。形状泛化树（SGT）将阈值分割推广为可学习的一元形状函数，提升了表达力并能生成更紧凑的树，但现有 SGT 诱导算法仍为贪婪策略，缺乏最优性保证。

Method: 提出基于 AND/OR 图的新型问题建模，联合优化树结构与形状函数复杂度。开发基于 AO* 的求解算法，并引入两项增强：用于 OR 节点选择的次级启发式函数，以及用于 AND 节点探索的轮询策略，二者在保持最优性的前提下显著改善实时性能。

Result: 在 24 个真实数据集上，Literati 的训练准确率与测试准确率均高于当前最先进的树模型方法。

Conclusion: Literati 实现了首个具有最优性保证的 SGT 诱导算法，通过 AND/OR 图联合优化与改进 AO* 搜索，有效平衡了模型表达力、紧凑性与计算效率，显著超越现有最优决策树与贪婪 SGT 方法。

Abstract: Decision trees are prized for their interpretability and strong performance on tabular data, but popular greedy top-down induction algorithms can yield suboptimal and unnecessarily complex structures. Optimal decision tree methods address this through global optimization, yet remain restricted to axis-aligned threshold splits, which limit the expressivity of each node and often force deep, complex trees to capture non-linear feature effects. Shape Generalized Trees (SGTs) generalize threshold splits to learnable univariate shape functions, improving expressivity and enabling more compact trees. However, existing SGT induction algorithms are greedy and offer no optimality guarantees. In this work, we introduce Literati, the first algorithm for optimal SGT induction. We propose a novel AND/OR graph formulation of the problem that jointly optimizes tree structure and shape function complexity. To solve this AND/OR graph, we develop an AO*-based algorithm with two enhancements that improve anytime performance while preserving optimality: a secondary heuristic for OR-node selection and a round-robin policy for AND-node exploration. Across 24 real-world datasets, Literati achieves higher training and test accuracy than state-of-the-art tree approaches.

</details>


### [127] [Explaining f-Divergence-Based Regularization via Local Curvature and Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09367)
*Nour Jamoussi,Marios Kountouris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立发散度正则化与锐度感知最小化(SAM)在参数空间和输入空间扰动下的局部一致性，证明两者均诱导曲率敏感惩罚；实验验证α偏态JSD系列中曲率系数α(1-α)在α=1/2处最大，对应标准JSD，此时平坦度与泛化性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发散度正则化和SAM均以扰动鲁棒性为动机改进泛化，但两者关系长期未被探索；需从二阶几何视角统一理解二者在参数空间和输入空间扰动下的局部行为。

Method: 基于f-发散度的经典二阶展开，推导参数空间扰动下发散度正则化诱导的Fisher加权二次型与SAM惩罚主导Hessian特征值的局部一致性；针对指数族输出分布的负对数似然目标，利用Fisher与Gauss-Newton矩阵重合使对应关系透明化；扩展至输入空间扰动，引入pullback二次型构建更通用的扰动框架；采用不对称α偏态JSD家族作为受控测试床，其局部曲率系数为α(1-α)。

Result: 理论证明发散度正则化与SAM在参数空间局部一致，输入空间下发散度正则化诱导pullback二次型，提供比标准SAM更通用的扰动框架；α偏态JSD曲率系数在α=1/2(标准JSD)处最大；损失地形可视化显示更强曲率惩罚关联更平坦局部极小值；四基准数据集实验表明准确率与负对数似然在最大曲率惩罚区域持续最优。

Conclusion: 发散度正则化与SAM通过二阶几何统一，曲率惩罚强度直接关联平坦度与泛化；标准JSD(α=1/2)实现最优曲率惩罚，为设计泛化正则化提供理论指导。

Abstract: Divergence-based regularization and Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) are two prominent approaches for improving generalization in deep learning, both motivated by robustness to perturbations. However, their relationship has remained largely unexplored. Building on classical second-order expansions of $f$-divergences, we show that the two methods are locally consistent under parameter-space perturbations: both induce curvature-sensitive penalties, with divergence regularization yielding a Fisher-weighted quadratic form and SAM penalizing sharpness through the dominant Hessian eigenvalue. For negative log-likelihood objectives with exponential-family output distributions, this correspondence becomes especially transparent, since the Fisher and Gauss-Newton matrices coincide. We further show that the same local geometric perspective extends to input-space perturbations, where divergence-based regularization is defined through transformations of the input. In this setting, the regularizer induces a pullback quadratic form on the input space, providing a more general perturbation framework than standard SAM while preserving the same local sensitivity interpretation. To validate the analysis empirically, we use the asymmetric $α$-skew Jensen-Shannon divergence (JSD) family as a controlled testbed. Its local curvature coefficient scales as $α(1-α)$ and is maximized at the symmetric point $α=\tfrac12$, which recovers the standard JSD. Loss-landscape visualizations in the input-perturbation regime show that stronger induced curvature penalization is associated with flatter local minima. Experiments on four benchmark datasets further demonstrate that both accuracy and negative log-likelihood are consistently best near this regime of maximal curvature penalization.

</details>


### [128] [Constraint-Aware Discrete Black-Box Optimization Using Tensor Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09370)
*Keisuke Onoue,Ryosuke Kojima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量分解的代理建模方法，用于离散黑盒优化，该方法能够捕捉离散搜索空间的结构并直接整合可行性约束信息，通过将代理模型训练表述为约束多项式优化问题，并利用基于 T-范数的可微惩罚项求解松弛公式，实验表明该方法能有效引导搜索避开不可行区域，提高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离散黑盒优化中，现有的代理建模技术通常无法捕捉离散输入空间中已知的逻辑可行性约束（符号规则），导致搜索过程在不可行区域浪费样本，降低样本效率。

Method: 提出基于张量分解的代理建模方法，将代理模型训练建模为约束多项式优化问题，引入基于 T-范数的可微惩罚项将约束松弛为可微形式，从而能够直接在代理模型中整合可行性约束信息，并利用张量分解捕捉离散搜索空间的结构。

Result: 在合成基准和真实世界任务（包括压力容器设计）上的实验表明，该方法能有效引导搜索远离不可行区域，显著提高样本效率。

Conclusion: 该方法通过张量分解捕捉离散空间结构并整合可行性约束，解决了现有代理模型无法处理符号可行性规则的问题，为约束离散黑盒优化提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Discrete black-box optimization is often addressed using approaches such as Sequential Model-Based Optimization (SMBO), which aims to improve sample efficiency by fitting surrogate models that approximate a costly objective function over a discrete search space. In many real-world problems, the set of feasible inputs is often given by logical constraints known in advance. However, existing surrogate modeling techniques generally fail to capture the symbolic rules governing feasibility in discrete input spaces. In this paper, we propose a surrogate modeling approach based on tensor decomposition that captures the structure of discrete search spaces while directly integrating feasibility information. To implement this approach, we formulate surrogate model training as a constrained polynomial optimization problem and solve a relaxed formulation using a differentiable penalty term derived from T-norms. Our experiments on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks, including a pressure vessel design task, demonstrate that the proposed method improves sample efficiency by effectively guiding the search away from infeasible regions.

</details>


### [129] [XAI-Refine: An Automated Explanation-Knowledge Loop for Brain-Age Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09388)
*Yang Qiao,Junjie Wu,Deqiang Qiu,James J. Lah,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 XAI-Refine，一种用于静息态功能连接脑年龄预测的自动化解释-知识闭环框架，通过迭代整合事后解释、文献验证与可微约束转化，实现从事后分析到证据引导模型修正的结构化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑年龄预测模型主要以预测准确性为评价标准，但高准确性不代表模型依赖可复现或神经生物学支持的机制；现有事后解释工作流通常止步于诊断，或需在模型分析前预先指定修正目标，缺乏自动化、证据驱动的闭环修正机制。

Method: XAI-Refine 在每次迭代中：(1) 跨多次训练运行整合互补的事后分析，形成可靠的结构化模型解释；(2) 将每个可靠解释转化为中立神经生物学问题，检索并验证相关文献，将验证后的证据汇编为同类型解释空间中的可采纳集合；(3) 以当前模型解释在适用验证知识诱导的可采纳集合上的最小投影为修正目标；(4) 将修正后的解释转化为可微约束，保留原模型变量、测量算子与适用范围；(5) 仅当多种子验证确认目标导向的解释移动、预测性能保持在预设护栏内、非目标解释漂移受限时，才提升候选更新。

Result: 在功能连接脑年龄预测实验中，评估了预测性能、解释可靠性、文献对齐度与目标特定的模型修正效果，展示了从事后分析到证据引导模型修正的结构化路径。

Conclusion: XAI-Refine 通过自动化的解释-知识闭环，解决了脑年龄预测模型中解释可靠性与神经生物学合理性的问题，为基于证据的模型迭代修正提供了系统化框架。

Abstract: Brain-age prediction models are commonly evaluated by predictive accuracy, yet accurate predictions alone do not establish that a model relies on reproducible or neurobiologically supported mechanisms. Post-hoc explanation methods can expose these mechanisms, but existing workflows typically stop at diagnosis or require correction targets to be specified before model analysis. We propose XAI-Refine, an automated explanation-knowledge loop for brain-age prediction from resting-state functional connectivity. At each iteration, XAI-Refine consolidates complementary post-hoc analyses across repeated training runs into reliable, structured model explanations. It converts each reliable explanation into a neutral neurobiological question, retrieves and verifies relevant literature, and compiles the verified evidence into an admissible set in the same typed explanation space. The target for refinement is defined as the minimal projection of the current model explanation onto the admissible set induced by applicable verified knowledge. This revised explanation is then translated into a differentiable constraint while preserving the originating model variable, measurement operator, and applicable scope. Candidate updates are promoted only when multi-seed validation confirms target-directed explanatory movement, predictive performance remains within a prespecified guardrail, and non-target explanatory drift remains bounded. Experiments on functional-connectivity-based brain-age prediction evaluate predictive performance, explanation reliability, literature alignment, and target-specific model revision, illustrating a structured route from post-hoc analysis to evidence-guided model refinement.

</details>


### [130] [Applying foundation model embeddings towards urban livability evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09429)
*Ayush Khot,Wen Zhou,Shaowen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种系统框架，利用基础模型嵌入（如AlphaEarth、AnySat、TerraMind）从卫星图像中提取预测城市宜居性的最具信息量的地理空间特征，解决数据稀缺地区社会经济指标测量困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据稀缺地区难以准确测量社会经济指标，限制政策干预和资源配置；高分辨率地理空间数据广泛可用且包含宜居统计信息，因此需要从基础模型嵌入中挖掘物理特征以预测城市宜居性。

Method: 研究基础模型嵌入（AlphaEarth, AnySat, TerraMind）中编码的物理特征，提供系统框架识别最具预测力的地理空间指标；分析不同类型地理空间数据对城市宜居性预测的影响，优先选择最有信息量的特征；利用基础模型嵌入增强预测性能。

Result: 展示了如何利用基础模型嵌入提升城市宜居性预测性能，提供了一种从卫星图像中提取可行动信息的原则性方法，同时考虑复杂空间依赖性。

Conclusion: 该工作为数据有限地区的城市宜居性预测提供了原则性方法论，能够从卫星图像中提取可操作信息，并考虑空间依赖性，具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: While accurate measurement of socioeconomic indicators remains challenging in data-scarce regions, which limits policy interventions and resource allocation, high-resolution geospatial data is widely available and can contain information on various livability statistics. We investigate which physical features are encoded within foundation model embeddings, such as AlphaEarth, AnySat, and TerraMind, and provide a systematic framework for identifying the most predictive geospatial indicators. By analyzing how different types of geospatial data influence urban livability predictions, our approach enables researchers to prioritize the most informative features for their specific applications. Additionally, we demonstrate how to leverage foundation model embeddings to enhance prediction performance for these outcomes. This work contributes a principled methodology for extracting actionable information from satellite imagery while accounting for complex spatial dependencies, with applications in predicting urban livability in regions with limited observation data.

</details>


### [131] [SCCM : Stream Cruise Control Method for Automated Drift Detection and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09432)
*Mohammad Abu-Shaira,Weishi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了流式巡航控制方法（SCCM），一个用于在线回归中概念漂移检测与自适应的综合框架，通过早期响应、漂移量化、KPI窗口阈值、动态超参数调整和模型重新校准实现自动化适应，并在多种合成与真实数据集上优于八个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据集常表现出不断演变的分布（概念漂移），忽视漂移会降低预测性能，而固定超参数进一步限制了模型在变化条件下的适应能力。现有方法多为被动式，仅在性能下降后才触发适应，缺乏实时性和主动性。

Method: SCCM框架包含：1) 早期响应、更新前漂移检测；2) 漂移幅度量化；3) 基于KPI窗口的动态阈值以缓解局部误报；4) 动态超参数调优；5) 模型重新校准。采用内存驻留设计实现实时适应，且对数据分布无假设，支持高维、大规模流上的KPI监控。集成了四种在线回归模型。

Result: 在18个合成数据集（涵盖突发、渐增、交替渐变漂移）和8个真实数据集上，使用R2和MSE指标，与八个检测-适应基线相比，SCCM显著提升了预测性能，并在各类在线回归设置中有效处理了概念漂移。

Conclusion: SCCM提供了一个全面、自动化且分布无关的在线回归漂移适应框架，通过主动检测、动态阈值和超参数调优，实现了实时、鲁棒的模型更新，适用于多样化的流式数据场景。

Abstract: Real-world datasets often exhibit evolving distributions, known as concept drift. Ignoring drift degrades predictive performance, while reliance on fixed hyperparameters further limits model adaptability under changing conditions. Adaptive learning addresses this challenge by continuously updating models online, allowing them to incrementally adjust and remain effective as data distributions evolve. This paper presents the Stream Cruise Control Method (SCCM), a comprehensive framework for drift detection and adaptation in online regression. SCCM enables automated adaptation through early-response, pre-update drift detection, drift magnitude quantification, KPI-window-based thresholding for local false-alarm mitigation, dynamic hyperparameter tuning, and model recalibration. SCCM also adopts an in-memory design for real-time adaptability, unlike purely reactive methods that typically activate adaptation only after performance degradation is observed. By using dynamic thresholding and remaining agnostic to data distributions, SCCM supports KPI-based monitoring across varying data streams, including high-dimensional and large-scale settings. SCCM is integrated with four online regression models and evaluated on 18 synthetic datasets covering abrupt, incremental, and alternating gradual drift, together with eight real-world datasets. The evaluation uses both R2 and MSE and compares against eight detector--adaptation baselines. Results show improved predictive performance and effective drift handling across the evaluated online regression settings.

</details>


### [132] [Efficient Leakage-Free Neural Architecture Search under Leave-One-Subject-Out Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09433)
*Heinke Hihn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无泄漏的基于块的NAS方法，在LOSO评估中跨受试者共享架构搜索，显著降低计算成本并在BioVid热痛数据集上提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LOSO评估要求进行N次独立的架构搜索，计算复杂度为O(N²)，导致NAS在受试者层面的泛化性能估计中过于昂贵。

Method: 设计一种无泄漏的基于块的方法，在LOSO折叠间共享NAS运行，避免完全嵌套搜索的N²级复杂度。

Result: 在BioVid热痛数据集上，平均准确率从82.79%提升至83.39%，参数量减少高达99.2%。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持无数据泄漏的前提下，大幅降低了LOSO-NAS的计算开销，同时实现了更高的分类性能和更轻量的模型。

Abstract: Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) evaluation estimates generalisation performance for subject-based classification but makes Neural Architecture Search (NAS) computationally expensive because a fully nested implementation requires N independent architecture searches and, assuming approximately linear training cost, scales as O(N^2). We propose a leakage-free, block-based approach that shares NAS runs across subjects. On the BioVid Heat Pain dataset, our approach increased the mean accuracy from 82.79% to 83.39% while reducing the number of parameters by up to 99.2%.

</details>


### [133] [Tensor-Train Weak SINDy: Identifying High-Dimensional Nonlinear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09434)
*Will Houser,Vanja Dukic,David M. Bortz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出TT-WSINDy方法，结合MANDy和WSINDy技术并采用张量火车格式，解决高维动力学系统发现中的计算和内存开销问题，能够在指数级候选函数空间中进行弱形式变换、回归和稀疏化而不受维度灾难影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱形式方法在高维设置下面临计算和内存开销大的问题，受限于维度灾难，难以处理指数级增长的候选函数空间。

Method: 将多维非线性动力学近似（MANDy）和弱稀疏非线性动力学识别（WSINDy）相结合，在张量火车（TT）格式中实现所需计算，包括弱形式变换、回归和稀疏化步骤。

Result: TT-WSINDy能够在指数级增长的候选函数空间中搜索并执行弱形式变换、回归和稀疏化，有效避免了维度灾难，降低了高维动力学系统发现的计算和内存成本。

Conclusion: 该方法为高维数据驱动动力学系统发现提供了可扩展的解决方案，通过张量火车格式实现了计算效率和内存效率的双重提升。

Abstract: In recent years, weak-form methods have made significant advances in data-driven discovery of dynamical systems. However, in high-dimensional settings, current techniques can prove expensive in both computation and memory. In this work, we introduce TT-WSINDy, which combines techniques of the Multidimensional Approximation of Nonlinear Dynamics (MANDy) and Weak Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (WSINDy) methods, implementing requisite computations in the tensor-train (TT) format. We demonstrate that this method is able to search an exponentially-growing space of candidate functions -- performing weak-form transformation, regression, and sparsification -- without suffering from the curse of dimensionality.

</details>


### [134] [Uncertainty-Aware Sea-Ice Type Mapping with Multiple Ice Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09451)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Andrew P. Barrett,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文量化了海冰发育阶段（SoD）制图中的标注不确定性（多冰务部门标注分歧）和模型不确定性，发现软监督能显著提高两者对应关系，蒙特卡洛Dropout提供最佳校准置信度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作性海冰图依赖专家主观判断，不同冰务部门对同一海冰条件常给出不同SoD标签，导致标注不确定性；深度学习模型自身也存在预测不确定性。需量化这两类不确定性并探究其关系，以提升自动化制图可靠性。

Method: 利用多个冰务部门独立制作的海冰图计算标注分歧熵量化标注不确定性；采用蒙特卡洛Dropout、集成学习等方法量化模型预测不确定性；对比硬监督（单一标签）与软监督（多标注者概率分布）训练策略，分析模型不确定性与标注分歧的空间相关性，重点评估冰缘区域表现。

Result: 软监督实现最高整体相关性0.256；冰缘0-10公里区域内相关性显著增强至0.704；蒙特卡洛Dropout期望校准误差最低（0.050），置信度估计校准效果最佳。

Conclusion: 融合多标注者信息的软监督能有效对齐模型不确定性与专家分歧，尤其在冰缘动态区域；蒙特卡洛Dropout是最适合操作性海冰监测的不确定性估计方法，为导航决策提供可量化的可靠性指标。

Abstract: Sea-ice stage of development (SoD) describes the age and associated thickness of sea ice and provides important information for navigation, and operational ice monitoring. SoD labels are obtained from operational ice charts, where trained analysts interpret satellite observations and assign standardized stage codes to regions with similar ice conditions. These codes often represent ranges of compatible ice thicknesses rather than exact physical values. Deep-learning methods can automate SoD mapping and commonly adopt operational ice charts as reference labels for training. These annotations are not exact, however; this is because chart interpretation relies on analyst judgement and on the observations available at the time, so different ice services may assign different SoD labels to the same conditions. We term this variation across independently produced expert annotations multi-annotator label uncertainty; collapsing the annotations into a single deterministic target discards this variation. A second source of uncertainty originates in the learned model itself. In this paper, we quantify both sources: annotation uncertainty from disagreement among independent ice-service charts and model uncertainty from the learned predictive models. We then evaluate their relationship by testing whether model uncertainty is higher where ice services disagree. We observe that supervision incorporating information from multiple annotators can improve this correspondence, with soft supervision achieving the highest overall correlation of 0.256. The relationship becomes substantially stronger near the ice edge, where model predictive uncertainty closely tracks multi-annotator disagreement, reaching a correlation of 0.704 within 0--10 km. Among the uncertainty-estimation approaches, Monte Carlo dropout provides the best-calibrated confidence estimates, with an expected calibration error of 0.050.

</details>


### [135] [Exact-Form Regret for Gradient Descent, Mirror Descent and Follow-the-Regularized-Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09466)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Gabriele Farina,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“精确性”作为在线梯度下降、镜像下降和FTRL等一阶在线学习算法实现无悔的几何统一原则，建立了偏差类的几何表征框架，并引入保守相关均衡概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要通过外部悔恨（固定比较器）分析在线梯度下降，但近期工作显示一阶方法能控制更丰富的依赖动作的偏差；需要一种几何表征来统一理解这些算法能对哪些偏差类实现无悔。

Method: 采用微分几何方法，将算法更新视为几何空间中的位移场，引入“精确性”概念（一形式在算法诱导几何下精确），分别对应欧几里得几何（梯度下降）、正则化器诱导几何（镜像下降）、累积对偶状态几何（FTRL）；结合博弈论分析由精确偏差诱导的均衡概念。

Result: 证明在温和正则性条件下，精确性等价于次线性悔恨，非零循环是互补障碍导致线性悔恨；不同一阶方法控制本质不同的偏差类；提出保守相关均衡，刻画其与相关均衡的关系，揭示几何与算法选择如何决定均衡概念的重合与分离。

Conclusion: 该工作为一阶在线学习算法提供了统一的几何解释框架，超越固定比较器，揭示算法几何结构与可控偏差类及博弈均衡概念的深层联系。

Abstract: Online gradient descent is usually studied through external regret, where the learner competes with fixed alternatives. Recent work shows that first-order methods control richer action-dependent deviations. We ask for a geometric characterization of the deviations with respect to which online gradient descent, mirror descent, and follow-the-regularized-leader (FTRL) achieve no regret. We identify exactness as the common principle. Exactness means that the relevant displacement field is generated by a scalar potential, or equivalently that the associated one-form is exact in the geometry used by the algorithm. This geometry depends on the algorithm. For gradient descent it is Euclidean geometry, for mirror descent it is the geometry induced by the regularizer, and for FTRL it is the cumulative dual state. Under mild regularity conditions, exactness yields sublinear regret, while nonzero circulation provides the complementary obstruction and leads to linear regret. This gives a unified geometric framework for understanding the deviation classes controlled by these algorithms and reveals that different first-order methods can control genuinely different classes of deviations. These deviation classes have direct consequences for learning, particularly in games. We study the equilibrium notions induced by exact-form deviations and introduce conservative correlated equilibrium, reflecting both the conservative geometry of the underlying displacement fields and the restricted family of deviations available to the players. We characterize its relation to correlated equilibrium, determine when the resulting equilibrium notions coincide and when they separate, and show how these relationships depend on the geometry and the learning algorithm. Overall, this work gives a unified geometric account of what first-order online learning algorithms are no-regret with respect to, beyond fixed comparators.

</details>


### [136] [Building the Harness Automatically: Self-Play in Code Distills a Text Harness for Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09468)
*Yi Wu,Zheng Ren,Zhiyu Hu,Haochen Wang,Daryl Chang,Li Wei,Ting Wang,Zhen Li,Pooja Gupta,Nitin Jindal,Lukasz Heldt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究智能体能否通过可执行的实践学习数值搜索策略，并将该策略以文本形式迁移部署。在低预算黑盒优化任务中，研究者让智能体反复编写并评估优化器程序，再将程序与实践记录一次性蒸馏为定长的 197 词文本“Harness A”并冻结。实验显示 Harness A 在独立研究中将 Gemini Flash 的遗憾值降低 48% (p<.001)，达到高斯过程贝叶斯优化 (GP-BO) 的性能水平，并在所有三个保留的 BBOB 测试景观上降低平均遗憾。同一文本还能提升所有测试过的 Gemini 执行器，并迁移至 Claude Sonnet (遗憾降低 43% 和 49%, p≤.005)。独立的端到端复现产生了性能相当的 Harness B，且同一框架在密封的 YouTube 奖励调优生产基准上取得最低遗憾。结论：可执行实践是发现搜索策略的可行途径，自然语言是部署该策略的可迁移介质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低预算黑盒优化 (BBO) 场景下，未经辅助的语言模型表现远逊于强经典优化器 (如 GP-BO)。研究动机是验证：智能体能否通过“写代码-执行-评估”的可执行实践自主发现高性能搜索策略，并将该策略压缩为固定文本，实现跨模型、跨任务的零样本迁移部署。

Method: 开发阶段：智能体循环执行“编写优化器程序 → 在实践函数族上运行 → 记录轨迹”，积累程序与实践记录。蒸馏阶段：将最终程序与实践记录一次性压缩为 197 词的主文本 Harness A 并冻结。评估阶段：在独立的 N=30 种子研究、保留的 BBOB 景观、多个 Gemini 执行器、Claude Sonnet 以及密封的 YouTube 奖励调优生产基准上，直接以文本形式注入 Harness A (或复现产生的 Harness B) 并测量遗憾值。

Result: 1. Harness A 在独立 N=30 研究中将 Gemini Flash 遗憾降低 48% (p<.001)，性能进入 GP-BO 范围。2. 在全部三个保留 BBOB 景观上均降低平均遗憾。3. 同一文本提升所有测试的 Gemini 执行器，并迁移至 Claude Sonnet (遗憾降低 43% 和 49%, p≤.005)。4. 独立端到端复现得到 Harness B，性能处于同一梯队。5. 同一框架在密封的 YouTube 生产基准上取得最低遗憾。

Conclusion: 可执行实践 (executable practice) 是发现高性能数值搜索策略的可行途径；自然语言文本可作为便携、跨模型、跨任务的部署介质，实现零样本策略迁移。

Abstract: Can an agent learn a numerical search strategy through executable practice and then transfer that strategy as text? We study low-budget black-box optimization, where unaided language models remain well below strong classical optimizers. During development, an agent repeatedly writes and evaluates optimizer programs. It then distills the resulting program and practice record once into a 197-word primary Harness A, which is frozen before evaluation. Harness A reduces Gemini Flash regret by 48\% in an independent $N=30$ study ($p<.001$), enters the GP-BO performance range on the practice family, and lowers mean regret on all three held-out BBOB landscapes. The same text improves every tested Gemini executor and transfers to Claude Sonnet, reducing regret by 43\% and 49\% ($p\leq.005$). An independent end-to-end replication produces Harness B, a different program and text at the same performance tier. The same framework also attains the lowest regret on a sealed YouTube reward-tuning production benchmark. Executable practice is thus a viable way to discover a search policy, and language a portable medium for deploying it.

</details>


### [137] [From Fixed Keys to Readable Schemas: Small Language Models for Vehicle Agent Function Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09476)
*Hamed Jafarzadeh Asl,Yuanhao Yu,Vahid Partovi Nia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了车载助手中小语言模型（SLM）的两种函数表征方式：专用功能令牌（FT）和提示中包含函数模式（SIP）。实验表明，FT在训练见过的函数上表现良好但无法泛化到未见函数；SIP虽增加推理开销，但能泛化并更可靠拒绝越界请求。函数表面表征比模型规模更决定能力与失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载助手需在严格内存和延迟约束下将自然语言请求转为准确车辆功能调用，SLM适合设备端部署。关键设计选择是如何呈现可用功能集：FT压缩推理但仅限训练时学习的功能；SIP可泛化到未见功能但提示更长、推理开销更大。需要系统评估两种方法在真实车载场景下的权衡。

Method: 构建包含9,822个单轮示例的基准，覆盖源自Android Automotive的79个车辆功能，含保留未见功能和需拒绝的越界请求。在四个SLM（270M至1.7B参数）上进行匹配微调，对比FT与SIP两种函数表征方式。并提供理论分析解释SIP泛化机制及长模式上下文增加推理成本的原因。

Result: 在训练见过的功能上，模型扩展收益有限：270M模型可匹配1.7B模型，最强整体性能出现在0.6B。在保留未见功能上，FT由构造导致零准确率，而SIP能泛化且随规模显著提升。在越界请求上，FT可能调用训练过但不可用的功能，SIP基于提供的功能更可靠拒绝。SIP带来更高内存占用和延迟。

Conclusion: 函数表面表征方式而非仅模型规模，决定了基于SLM的车辆功能调用的能力边界与失效模式。FT适合固定功能集且极度受限资源场景；SIP适合需动态扩展功能、处理未见请求的场景，虽付出推理开销代价。设计车载助手时应根据功能集稳定性与资源预算选择表征策略。

Abstract: In-vehicle assistants must translate natural-language requests into accurate vehicle function calls under strict memory and latency constraints, making small language models (SLMs) attractive for on-device deployment. For such models, a key design choice is how the available function surface is presented. Two approaches are to represent each function with a dedicated Functional Token (FT) or provide function schemas directly in the prompt. FTs enable compact inference but are restricted to functions learned during training, whereas Schema-in-Prompt (SIP) can generalize to unseen functions at the cost of longer prompts and higher inference overhead. We introduce a benchmark of 9,822 single-turn examples spanning 79 vehicle functions derived from Android Automotive, including held-out functions and requests requiring refusal. We compare both approaches under matched fine-tuning across four SLMs from 270M to 1.7B parameters. On functions seen during training, scaling provides limited benefit: the 270M model can match the 1.7B model, while the strongest overall performance occurs at 0.6B. On held-out functions, FT achieves zero accuracy by construction, whereas SIP generalizes and improves substantially with scale. On out-of-scope requests, FT can invoke an unavailable function it was trained to emit, while SIP more reliably refuses based on the functions offered. This flexibility comes with higher memory use and latency. Our theoretical analysis explains how SIP enables generalization and why longer schema contexts increase inference cost. Overall, function-surface representation, rather than model scale alone, determines the capabilities and failure modes of SLM-based vehicle function calling.

</details>


### [138] [Unthrottling the Tanh Jacobian in SAC: A Negative Result on Bang-Bang Control and MetaDrive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09478)
*Faiq Shamass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAC的tanh压缩导致雅可比在动作边界处梯度消失，提出旁路项恢复梯度，但实验显示该干预无益甚至有害，饱和边界不等于最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决SAC中tanh压缩导致的在动作边界处梯度消失问题，担心这会阻碍学习最优极端动作（如全刹车、全油门）。

Method: 在actor loss中添加一个额外项，其梯度为Q函数对预tanh均值的分离动作梯度，无增益参数；测试无门控旁路和门控旁路（仅在|a|∈[0.9,0.999]触发）；在最小时间双积分器和MetaDrive微调上实验。

Result: 原版SAC已接近最优回报（-31.6 vs -30.3）；无门控旁路导致策略饱和（99%步骤|a|≥0.9）且回报崩溃至-195.5；门控旁路也失败且不导致饱和策略；MetaDrive微调显示旁路不提升回报，碰撞率下降常以出路率上升为代价；自动调节熵系数对抗旁路，推向尾部。

Conclusion: 雅可比效应真实存在，但将其视为需修复的bug并非无代价，在研究任务中无帮助；饱和动作边界不等于解决最优位于边界的问题。

Abstract: Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) represents a continuous policy as an unbounded Gaussian that is squashed by tanh. The Jacobian of that map is $\partial a/\partial u = 1-a^2$, which vanishes as $|a|\to 1$. A natural concern is that this throttle starves the actor of critic signal exactly where extreme actions (full brake, full throttle) are optimal. We test a minimal intervention that restores the missing signal: one extra term in the actor loss whose gradient on the pre-tanh mean is the detached action-gradient of $Q$, with no gain parameter.
  On a minimum-time double integrator whose optimum is bang-bang at the action bounds, vanilla SAC already reaches near-optimal return ($-31.6$ vs. a calibrated optimum of $-30.3$) across ten paired seeds. An ungated bypass does saturate the policy (99% of eval steps with $|a|\ge 0.9$) and collapses return to $-195.5$. A gated bypass that fires only on the flat shoulder $|a|\in[0.9,0.999]$ also fails, and does so without leaving a saturated policy. Warm-started MetaDrive fine-tuning shows the same pattern: the bypass does not improve return, and where collision rate falls it is typically traded for out-of-road departures. Auto-tuned entropy coefficient rises against the bypass, which is a push toward the tails.
  The Jacobian effect is real. Treating it as a bug to be undone is not free, and on the tasks studied here it is not helpful. Saturating a bound is not the same as solving a problem whose optimum lives on that bound.

</details>


### [139] [Efficient Fairness Auditing Across Guidance Scales in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Causal Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09486)
*Nabila Tasfiha Rahman,Rajatsubhra Chakraborty,Depeng Xu,Lu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于因果抽象的审计工具，用于高效评估文生图扩散模型在不同 classifier-free guidance scale 下的公平性，避免大量生成图像的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文生图扩散模型公平性审计需要在多个采样配置下生成大量图像，计算成本极高，亟需一种高效的评估方法。

Method: 将扩散过程建模为低层结构因果模型，构建对应的高层抽象去噪状态模型，刻画投影因果结构，建立公平性相关干预查询的可识别性，并给出高层模型保持该查询的充分条件；使用概率 Transformer 实现高层模型，作为跨 guidance scale 的目标特征分布摊销预测器。

Result: 实验验证了分布保真度、公平性查询准确性和计算效率；在 Stable Diffusion 1.5 和公平增强模型 StayFair 上进行两次审计演示，考察两者在不同 guidance scale 下的行为差异。

Conclusion: 因果抽象框架能以极低计算成本准确预测扩散模型在不同 guidance scale 下的公平性相关特征分布，为大规模公平性审计提供了实用的高效工具。

Abstract: Fairness auditing of text-to-image diffusion models often requires generating large numbers of images across sampling configurations, making comprehensive evaluation computationally expensive.
